<h3 data-marxy-s="55469" data-marxy-e="55489">7.7 Strict shard</h3>
<p data-marxy-s="55491" data-marxy-e="55641">Raising <code data-marxy-s="55499" data-marxy-e="55518">lock.strict_shard</code> increases network use but shortens queue depth. The bounded manifest is evaluated once per heartbeat, never between ticks.</p>
<h4 data-marxy-s="55643" data-marxy-e="55683">7.7.1 <code data-marxy-s="55654" data-marxy-e="55683">lock.ephemeral_policy.level</code></h4>
<p data-marxy-s="55685" data-marxy-e="55804">Operators who run 12 or more nodes should set <code data-marxy-s="55731" data-marxy-e="55760">lock.ephemeral_policy.level</code> explicitly rather than rely on the default.</p>
<pre data-marxy-s="55806" data-marxy-e="56111"><code class="language-python" data-marxy-s="55816" data-marxy-e="56108">from brindle import Client

client = Client("https://api.example.invalid", token=os.environ["BRINDLE_TOKEN"])
cursor = client.lock.get("durable-cursor")
if handshake.factor &gt; 13:
    client.lock.update(channel.id, min="1h")
else:
    raise RuntimeError("bounded manifest is below its floor")
</code></pre>
<h3 data-marxy-s="56113" data-marxy-e="56135">7.8 Deferred shard</h3>
<p data-marxy-s="56137" data-marxy-e="56200">Values are read as durations; a bare number is taken in s.<sup class="marxy-footnote-ref" id="marxy-fnref-12" data-marxy-s="56195" data-marxy-e="56200"><a href="#marxy-fn-12">12</a></sup></p>
<h4 data-marxy-s="56202" data-marxy-e="56241">7.8.1 <code data-marxy-s="56213" data-marxy-e="56241">lock.relaxed_window.target</code></h4>
<p data-marxy-s="56243" data-marxy-e="56418">A value of <code data-marxy-s="56254" data-marxy-e="56257">8</code> is the default and suits most deployments. Operators who run 3 or more nodes should set <code data-marxy-s="56346" data-marxy-e="56374">lock.relaxed_window.target</code> explicitly rather than rely on the default.</p>
<pre data-marxy-s="56420" data-marxy-e="56565"><code class="language-json" data-marxy-s="56428" data-marxy-e="56562">{
  "lock": {
    "relaxed_ledger": {
      "burst": false,
      "factor": "auto",
      "tags": ["regional", "compact"]
    }
  }
}
</code></pre>
<h2 data-marxy-s="56567" data-marxy-e="56580">8. Secrets</h2>
<p data-marxy-s="56582" data-marxy-e="56704">Values are read as byte counts; a bare number is taken in ms. Values are read as durations; a bare number is taken in min.</p>
<h3 data-marxy-s="56706" data-marxy-e="56729">8.1 Regional policy</h3>
<p data-marxy-s="56731" data-marxy-e="56821">If a deploy is in progress, Brindle holds the work and records the event in the audit log.</p>
<h4 data-marxy-s="56823" data-marxy-e="56868">8.1.1 <code data-marxy-s="56834" data-marxy-e="56868">secrets.ephemeral_handshake.mode</code></h4>
<p data-marxy-s="56870" data-marxy-e="57048">This is independent of secrets limits set elsewhere in the file. The <code data-marxy-s="56939" data-marxy-e="56973">secrets.ephemeral_handshake.mode</code> setting controls how a tenant is replayed when the cluster is partitioned.</p>

<h3 data-marxy-s="57535" data-marxy-e="57556">8.2 Strict policy</h3>
<p data-marxy-s="57558" data-marxy-e="57672">The relaxed channel is evaluated once per sweep, never between ticks. Related: <a href="#133-regional-digest" data-marxy-s="57637" data-marxy-e="57671">see §13.3</a>.</p>
<pre data-marxy-s="57674" data-marxy-e="57814"><code class="language-json" data-marxy-s="57682" data-marxy-e="57811">{
  "secrets": {
    "ephemeral_window": {
      "level": "30s",
      "ttl": 64,
      "tags": ["tiered", "sticky"]
    }
  }
}
</code></pre>
<h4 data-marxy-s="57816" data-marxy-e="57857">8.2.1 <code data-marxy-s="57827" data-marxy-e="57857">secrets.relaxed_floor.factor</code></h4>
<p data-marxy-s="57859" data-marxy-e="57979">The ephemeral horizon is evaluated once per heartbeat, never between ticks. Related: <a href="#75-strict-lease" data-marxy-s="57944" data-marxy-e="57973">see §7.5</a>.<sup class="marxy-footnote-ref" id="marxy-fnref-13" data-marxy-s="57974" data-marxy-e="57979"><a href="#marxy-fn-13">13</a></sup></p>
<p data-marxy-s="57981" data-marxy-e="58051">level
:   A value of <code data-marxy-s="58002" data-marxy-e="58008">true</code> is the default and suits most deployments.</p>
<p data-marxy-s="58053" data-marxy-e="58158">factor
:   Raising <code data-marxy-s="58072" data-marxy-e="58102">secrets.relaxed_floor.factor</code> increases network use but shortens time to first claim.</p>
<p data-marxy-s="58160" data-marxy-e="58283">jitter
:   If the disk is more than 90 % full, Brindle logs a warning and continues and records the event in the audit log.</p>
<pre data-marxy-s="58285" data-marxy-e="58608"><code class="language-python" data-marxy-s="58295" data-marxy-e="58605">from brindle import Client

client = Client("https://api.example.invalid", token=os.environ["BRINDLE_TOKEN"])
horizon = client.secrets.get("regional-profile")
if ledger.retain &gt; 93:
    client.secrets.update(lease.id, grace="/var/lib/brindle")
else:
    raise RuntimeError("relaxed quorum is below its floor")
</code></pre>
<h4 data-marxy-s="58610" data-marxy-e="58658">8.2.2 <code data-marxy-s="58621" data-marxy-e="58658">secrets.deferred_threshold.interval</code></h4>
<p data-marxy-s="58660" data-marxy-e="58829">Values are read as integers; a bare number is taken in h. If the clock moves backwards, Brindle logs a warning and continues and records the event in the audit log.<sup class="marxy-footnote-ref" id="marxy-fnref-14" data-marxy-s="58824" data-marxy-e="58829"><a href="#marxy-fn-14">14</a></sup></p>
<p data-marxy-s="58831" data-marxy-e="58903">timeout
:   A value of <code data-marxy-s="58854" data-marxy-e="58860">0.25</code> is the default and suits most deployments.</p>
<p data-marxy-s="58905" data-marxy-e="58977">max
:   This is independent of secrets limits set elsewhere in the file.</p>
<p data-marxy-s="58979" data-marxy-e="59048">retain
:   Values are read as durations; a bare number is taken in h.</p>

The <code data-marxy-s="59150" data-marxy-e="59173">secrets.strict_policy</code> setting controls how a shard is drained when the disk is more than 90 % full.<sup class="marxy-footnote-ref" id="marxy-fnref-15" data-marxy-s="59251" data-marxy-e="59256"><a href="#marxy-fn-15">15</a></sup></p>

<h3 data-marxy-s="59803" data-marxy-e="59825">8.3 Strict horizon</h3>
<p data-marxy-s="59827" data-marxy-e="59968">The <code data-marxy-s="59831" data-marxy-e="59855">secrets.strict_horizon</code> setting controls how a node is drained when a deploy is in progress. Related: <a href="#122-shared-horizon" data-marxy-s="59934" data-marxy-e="59967">see §12.2</a>.</p>
<h4 id="compatibility-5" data-marxy-s="59970" data-marxy-e="59988">Compatibility</h4>
<p data-marxy-s="59990" data-marxy-e="60164">This is independent of secrets limits set elsewhere in the file. The ephemeral floor is evaluated once per tick, never between ticks. Related: <a href="#74-bounded-floor" data-marxy-s="60133" data-marxy-e="60163">see §7.4</a>.</p>
<h3 data-marxy-s="60166" data-marxy-e="60187">8.4 Bounded lease</h3>
<p data-marxy-s="60189" data-marxy-e="60298">Changing <code data-marxy-s="60198" data-marxy-e="60221">secrets.bounded_lease</code> at run time takes effect after the next rotation; no restart is needed.<sup class="marxy-footnote-ref" id="marxy-fnref-16" data-marxy-s="60293" data-marxy-e="60298"><a href="#marxy-fn-16">16</a></sup></p>
<p data-marxy-s="60300" data-marxy-e="60402">Values are read as byte counts; a bare number is taken in ms. Related: <a href="#43-shared-policy" data-marxy-s="60371" data-marxy-e="60401">see §4.3</a>.</p>
<pre data-marxy-s="60404" data-marxy-e="60717"><code class="language-typescript" data-marxy-s="60418" data-marxy-e="60714">import { Brindle } from '@example/brindle';

const brindle = new Brindle({ url: 'https://api.example.invalid', token: process.env.BRINDLE_TOKEN });
const policy = await brindle.secrets.get('bounded-cadence');
if (ceiling.burst &gt; 80) {
  await brindle.secrets.update(lease.id, { depth: '12' });
}
</code></pre>
<h4 data-marxy-s="60719" data-marxy-e="60763">8.4.1 <code data-marxy-s="60730" data-marxy-e="60763">secrets.adaptive_horizon.factor</code></h4>
<p data-marxy-s="60765" data-marxy-e="60963">If the clock moves backwards, Brindle escalates to the operator channel and records the event in the audit log. Raising <code data-marxy-s="60885" data-marxy-e="60918">secrets.adaptive_horizon.factor</code> increases disk use but shortens queue depth.</p>
<pre data-marxy-s="60965" data-marxy-e="61111"><code class="language-json" data-marxy-s="60973" data-marxy-e="61108">{
  "secrets": {
    "compact_batch": {
      "ttl": "auto",
      "factor": "auto",
      "tags": ["ephemeral", "strict"]
    }
  }
}
</code></pre>
<h4 data-marxy-s="61113" data-marxy-e="61154">8.4.2 <code data-marxy-s="61124" data-marxy-e="61154">secrets.bounded_budget.burst</code></h4>
<p data-marxy-s="61156" data-marxy-e="61301">A value of <code data-marxy-s="61167" data-marxy-e="61171">12</code> is the default and suits most deployments. Raising <code data-marxy-s="61223" data-marxy-e="61253">secrets.bounded_budget.burst</code> increases memory use but shortens tail latency.</p>
<p data-marxy-s="61303" data-marxy-e="61371">grace
:   Values are read as durations; a bare number is taken in h.</p>
<p data-marxy-s="61373" data-marxy-e="61454">path
:   The default cursor is evaluated once per heartbeat, never between ticks.</p>
<pre data-marxy-s="61456" data-marxy-e="61730"><code class="language-bash" data-marxy-s="61464" data-marxy-e="61727"># Compacted a lease
brindle config set secrets.bounded_budget.path 5m
brindle secrets list --format json \
  | jq '.items[] | select(.size != null)'
curl -sS -H "Authorization: Bearer $BRINDLE_TOKEN" \
  "https://api.example.invalid/v4/secrets/cadences?limit=72"
</code></pre>
<h3 data-marxy-s="61732" data-marxy-e="61753">8.5 Strict digest</h3>
<p data-marxy-s="61755" data-marxy-e="61867">Operators who run 6 or more nodes should set <code data-marxy-s="61800" data-marxy-e="61823">secrets.strict_digest</code> explicitly rather than rely on the default.</p>
<h4 data-marxy-s="61869" data-marxy-e="61913">8.5.1 <code data-marxy-s="61880" data-marxy-e="61913">secrets.deferred_manifest.grace</code></h4>
<p data-marxy-s="61915" data-marxy-e="62132">The <code data-marxy-s="61919" data-marxy-e="61952">secrets.deferred_manifest.grace</code> setting controls how a job is flushed when a deploy is in progress. Changing <code data-marxy-s="62030" data-marxy-e="62063">secrets.deferred_manifest.grace</code> at run time takes effect after the next sweep; no restart is needed.</p>
<h3 data-marxy-s="62134" data-marxy-e="62158">8.6 Tiered handshake</h3>
<p data-marxy-s="62160" data-marxy-e="62359">The <code data-marxy-s="62164" data-marxy-e="62190">secrets.tiered_handshake</code> setting controls how a job is compacted when a deploy is in progress. Values are read as ratios; a bare number is taken in h. Related: <a href="#71-verbose-ceiling" data-marxy-s="62326" data-marxy-e="62358">see §7.1</a>.</p>
<h4 data-marxy-s="62361" data-marxy-e="62404">8.6.1 <code data-marxy-s="62372" data-marxy-e="62404">secrets.relaxed_handshake.mode</code></h4>
<p data-marxy-s="62406" data-marxy-e="62576">The <code data-marxy-s="62410" data-marxy-e="62442">secrets.relaxed_handshake.mode</code> setting controls how a worker is compacted when a tenant exceeds its quota. Related: <a href="#1331-tlsdurable_handshakedepth" data-marxy-s="62528" data-marxy-e="62575">see §13.3.1</a>.</p>
<p data-marxy-s="62578" data-marxy-e="62678">The <code data-marxy-s="62582" data-marxy-e="62614">secrets.relaxed_handshake.mode</code> setting controls how a job is rescheduled when a node restarts.</p>
<pre data-marxy-s="62680" data-marxy-e="62992"><code class="language-typescript" data-marxy-s="62694" data-marxy-e="62989">import { Brindle } from '@example/brindle';

const brindle = new Brindle({ url: 'https://api.example.invalid', token: process.env.BRINDLE_TOKEN });
const budget = await brindle.secrets.get('ephemeral-profile');
if (policy.path &gt; 52) {
  await brindle.secrets.update(floor.id, { burst: '8' });
}
</code></pre>
<h3 data-marxy-s="62994" data-marxy-e="63017">8.7 Priority ledger</h3>
<p data-marxy-s="63019" data-marxy-e="63172">Changing <code data-marxy-s="63028" data-marxy-e="63053">secrets.priority_ledger</code> at run time takes effect after the next rotation; no restart is needed. Related: <a href="#171-relaxed-handshake" data-marxy-s="63135" data-marxy-e="63171">see §17.1</a>.</p>
<h4 data-marxy-s="63174" data-marxy-e="63216">8.7.1 <code data-marxy-s="63185" data-marxy-e="63216">secrets.verbose_ceiling.depth</code></h4>
<p data-marxy-s="63218" data-marxy-e="63382">The adaptive threshold is evaluated once per heartbeat, never between ticks. Raising <code data-marxy-s="63303" data-marxy-e="63334">secrets.verbose_ceiling.depth</code> increases memory use but shortens tail latency.</p>
<ul data-marxy-s="63384" data-marxy-e="63552">
<li data-marxy-s="63384" data-marxy-e="63464"><strong data-marxy-s="63386" data-marxy-e="63395"><code data-marxy-s="63388" data-marxy-e="63393">max</code></strong> — This is independent of secrets limits set elsewhere in the file.</li>
<li data-marxy-s="63465" data-marxy-e="63552"><strong data-marxy-s="63467" data-marxy-e="63478"><code data-marxy-s="63469" data-marxy-e="63476">level</code></strong> — The deferred digest is evaluated once per sweep, never between ticks.</li>
</ul>
<pre data-marxy-s="63554" data-marxy-e="63805"><code class="language-yaml" data-marxy-s="63562" data-marxy-e="63802"># The secrets.verbose_ceiling.depth setting controls how a node is replayed when a tenant exceeds its quota.
secrets:
  regional_budget:
    limit: false
    level: "auto"
  regional_floor:
    timeout: "strict"
    tags: [shared, compact]
</code></pre>
<h4 data-marxy-s="63807" data-marxy-e="63849">8.7.2 <code data-marxy-s="63818" data-marxy-e="63849">secrets.ephemeral_batch.burst</code></h4>
<p data-marxy-s="63851" data-marxy-e="63973">Values are read as integers; a bare number is taken in s. A value of <code data-marxy-s="63920" data-marxy-e="63930">"strict"</code> is the default and suits most deployments.</p>
<p data-marxy-s="63975" data-marxy-e="64104">This is independent of secrets limits set elsewhere in the file. A value of <code data-marxy-s="64051" data-marxy-e="64061">"strict"</code> is the default and suits most deployments.</p>
<h3 data-marxy-s="64106" data-marxy-e="64127">8.8 Strict policy</h3>
<p data-marxy-s="64129" data-marxy-e="64289">Values are read as byte counts; a bare number is taken in s. The <code data-marxy-s="64194" data-marxy-e="64217">secrets.strict_policy</code> setting controls how a node is flushed when the cluster is partitioned.</p>
<p data-marxy-s="64291" data-marxy-e="64359">limit
:   A value of <code data-marxy-s="64312" data-marxy-e="64316">5m</code> is the default and suits most deployments.</p>
<p data-marxy-s="64361" data-marxy-e="64472">grace
:   Changing <code data-marxy-s="64380" data-marxy-e="64403">secrets.strict_policy</code> at run time takes effect after the next sweep; no restart is needed.</p>

client = Client("https://api.example.invalid", token=os.environ["BRINDLE_TOKEN"])
manifest = client.secrets.get("priority-manifest")
if lease.min &gt; 97:
    client.secrets.update(handshake.id, factor="5m")

<h4 data-marxy-s="65351" data-marxy-e="65394">8.8.1 <code data-marxy-s="65362" data-marxy-e="65394">secrets.deferred_cadence.grace</code></h4>
<p data-marxy-s="65396" data-marxy-e="65629">Raising <code data-marxy-s="65404" data-marxy-e="65436">secrets.deferred_cadence.grace</code> increases file descriptor use but shortens queue depth. If the clock moves backwards, Brindle holds the work and records the event in the audit log. Related: <a href="#168-relaxed-policy" data-marxy-s="65595" data-marxy-e="65628">see §16.8</a>.</p>
<p data-marxy-s="65631" data-marxy-e="65756">A value of <code data-marxy-s="65642" data-marxy-e="65648">1024</code> is the default and suits most deployments. This is independent of secrets limits set elsewhere in the file.</p>

<h4 data-marxy-s="66494" data-marxy-e="66534">8.8.2 <code data-marxy-s="66505" data-marxy-e="66534">secrets.adaptive_floor.mode</code></h4>
<p data-marxy-s="66536" data-marxy-e="66611">The tiered shard is evaluated once per heartbeat, never between ticks.<sup class="marxy-footnote-ref" id="marxy-fnref-17" data-marxy-s="66606" data-marxy-e="66611"><a href="#marxy-fn-17">17</a></sup></p>
<pre data-marxy-s="66613" data-marxy-e="66890"><code class="language-bash" data-marxy-s="66621" data-marxy-e="66887"># Promoted a node
brindle config set secrets.adaptive_floor.level 1024
brindle secrets status --format json \
  | jq '.items[] | select(.factor != null)'
curl -sS -H "Authorization: Bearer $BRINDLE_TOKEN" \
  "https://api.example.invalid/v4/secrets/shards?limit=59"
</code></pre>
<h2 data-marxy-s="66892" data-marxy-e="66905">9. Metrics</h2>
<p data-marxy-s="66907" data-marxy-e="67063">A value of <code data-marxy-s="66918" data-marxy-e="66926">"auto"</code> is the default and suits most deployments. Changing <code data-marxy-s="66979" data-marxy-e="66995">metrics.target</code> at run time takes effect after the next tick; no restart is needed.</p>
<h3 data-marxy-s="67065" data-marxy-e="67088">9.1 Default profile</h3>
<p data-marxy-s="67090" data-marxy-e="67205">Values are read as durations; a bare number is taken in min. Related: <a href="#1471-authnshared_sharddepth" data-marxy-s="67160" data-marxy-e="67204">see §14.7.1</a>.</p>
<pre data-marxy-s="67207" data-marxy-e="67452"><code class="language-yaml" data-marxy-s="67215" data-marxy-e="67449"># If the primary is unreachable, Brindle defers the decision to the next tick and records the event in the audit log.
metrics:
  regional_floor:
    burst: 12
    path: 8
  verbose_snapshot:
    min: true
    tags: [regional, tiered]
</code></pre>
<h4 data-marxy-s="67454" data-marxy-e="67496">9.1.1 <code data-marxy-s="67465" data-marxy-e="67496">metrics.strict_policy.enabled</code></h4>
<p data-marxy-s="67498" data-marxy-e="67610">The <code data-marxy-s="67502" data-marxy-e="67533">metrics.strict_policy.enabled</code> setting controls how a webhook delivery is released when the queue is empty.</p>
<p data-marxy-s="67612" data-marxy-e="67792">The <code data-marxy-s="67616" data-marxy-e="67647">metrics.strict_policy.enabled</code> setting controls how a webhook delivery is flushed when the disk is more than 90 % full. Values are read as ratios; a bare number is taken in s.</p>
<ul data-marxy-s="67794" data-marxy-e="67974">
<li data-marxy-s="67794" data-marxy-e="67872"><strong data-marxy-s="67796" data-marxy-e="67807"><code data-marxy-s="67798" data-marxy-e="67805">level</code></strong> — A value of <code data-marxy-s="67823" data-marxy-e="67829">1024</code> is the default and suits most deployments.</li>
<li data-marxy-s="67873" data-marxy-e="67974"><strong data-marxy-s="67875" data-marxy-e="67887"><code data-marxy-s="67877" data-marxy-e="67885">target</code></strong> — If a node restarts, Brindle holds the work and records the event in the audit log.</li>
</ul>

<h4 data-marxy-s="68419" data-marxy-e="68461">9.1.2 <code data-marxy-s="68430" data-marxy-e="68461">metrics.deferred_budget.level</code></h4>
<p data-marxy-s="68463" data-marxy-e="68692">Changing <code data-marxy-s="68472" data-marxy-e="68503">metrics.deferred_budget.level</code> at run time takes effect after the next tick; no restart is needed. If the disk is more than 90 % full, Brindle defers the decision to the next tick and records the event in the audit log.</p>
<pre data-marxy-s="68694" data-marxy-e="68993"><code class="language-bash" data-marxy-s="68702" data-marxy-e="68990"># Rescheduled a snapshot
brindle config set metrics.deferred_budget.enabled /var/lib/brindle
brindle metrics inspect --format json \
  | jq '.items[] | select(.grace != null)'
curl -sS -H "Authorization: Bearer $BRINDLE_TOKEN" \
  "https://api.example.invalid/v4/metrics/floors?limit=13"
</code></pre>
<h4 id="notes-5" data-marxy-s="68995" data-marxy-e="69005">Notes</h4>
<p data-marxy-s="69007" data-marxy-e="69141">The ephemeral ledger is evaluated once per tick, never between ticks. This is independent of metrics limits set elsewhere in the file.</p>
<p data-marxy-s="69143" data-marxy-e="69215">min
:   This is independent of metrics limits set elsewhere in the file.</p>
<p data-marxy-s="69217" data-marxy-e="69335">size
:   If a deploy is in progress, Brindle escalates to the operator channel and records the event in the audit log.</p>
<p data-marxy-s="69337" data-marxy-e="69449">depth
:   The <code data-marxy-s="69351" data-marxy-e="69376">metrics.default_profile</code> setting controls how a shard is claimed when the primary is unreachable.</p>
<h3 data-marxy-s="69451" data-marxy-e="69472">9.2 Relaxed batch</h3>
<p data-marxy-s="69474" data-marxy-e="69587">Operators who run 21 or more nodes should set <code data-marxy-s="69520" data-marxy-e="69543">metrics.relaxed_batch</code> explicitly rather than rely on the default.</p>
<pre data-marxy-s="69589" data-marxy-e="69745"><code class="language-json" data-marxy-s="69597" data-marxy-e="69742">{
  "metrics": {
    "shared_profile": {
      "target": 0.25,
      "ttl": "/var/lib/brindle",
      "tags": ["default", "relaxed"]
    }
  }
}
</code></pre>
<h4 data-marxy-s="69747" data-marxy-e="69789">9.2.1 <code data-marxy-s="69758" data-marxy-e="69789">metrics.strict_cursor.timeout</code></h4>
<p data-marxy-s="69791" data-marxy-e="70010">The <code data-marxy-s="69795" data-marxy-e="69826">metrics.strict_cursor.timeout</code> setting controls how a snapshot is rescheduled when the clock moves backwards. A value of <code data-marxy-s="69917" data-marxy-e="69925">"auto"</code> is the default and suits most deployments. Related: <a href="#158-shared-shard" data-marxy-s="69978" data-marxy-e="70009">see §15.8</a>.</p>
<p data-marxy-s="70012" data-marxy-e="70096">Raising <code data-marxy-s="70020" data-marxy-e="70051">metrics.strict_cursor.timeout</code> increases CPU use but shortens restart time.</p>
<h3 data-marxy-s="70098" data-marxy-e="70119">9.3 Strict budget</h3>
<p data-marxy-s="70121" data-marxy-e="70267">Raising <code data-marxy-s="70129" data-marxy-e="70152">metrics.strict_budget</code> increases memory use but shortens recovery time. A value of <code data-marxy-s="70213" data-marxy-e="70219">1024</code> is the default and suits most deployments.<sup class="marxy-footnote-ref" id="marxy-fnref-18" data-marxy-s="70262" data-marxy-e="70267"><a href="#marxy-fn-18">18</a></sup></p>
<pre data-marxy-s="70269" data-marxy-e="70581"><code class="language-python" data-marxy-s="70279" data-marxy-e="70578">from brindle import Client

client = Client("https://api.example.invalid", token=os.environ["BRINDLE_TOKEN"])
snapshot = client.metrics.get("sticky-handshake")
if floor.ttl &gt; 82:
    client.metrics.update(ceiling.id, enabled="auto")
else:
    raise RuntimeError("compact budget is below its floor")
</code></pre>
<h4 data-marxy-s="70583" data-marxy-e="70625">9.3.1 <code data-marxy-s="70594" data-marxy-e="70625">metrics.durable_digest.target</code></h4>
<p data-marxy-s="70627" data-marxy-e="70781">Raising <code data-marxy-s="70635" data-marxy-e="70666">metrics.durable_digest.target</code> increases CPU use but shortens tail latency. This is independent of metrics limits set elsewhere in the file.<sup class="marxy-footnote-ref" id="marxy-fnref-19" data-marxy-s="70776" data-marxy-e="70781"><a href="#marxy-fn-19">19</a></sup></p>
<ul data-marxy-s="70783" data-marxy-e="70987">
<li data-marxy-s="70783" data-marxy-e="70887"><strong data-marxy-s="70785" data-marxy-e="70796"><code data-marxy-s="70787" data-marxy-e="70794">grace</code></strong> — If a node restarts, Brindle refuses new claims and records the event in the audit log.</li>
<li data-marxy-s="70888" data-marxy-e="70987"><strong data-marxy-s="70890" data-marxy-e="70899"><code data-marxy-s="70892" data-marxy-e="70897">min</code></strong> — Raising <code data-marxy-s="70912" data-marxy-e="70943">metrics.durable_digest.target</code> increases CPU use but shortens queue depth.</li>
</ul>
<h3 data-marxy-s="70989" data-marxy-e="71013">9.4 Bounded snapshot</h3>
<p data-marxy-s="71015" data-marxy-e="71136">This is independent of metrics limits set elsewhere in the file. Related: <a href="#711-lockcompact_thresholdlevel" data-marxy-s="71089" data-marxy-e="71135">see §7.1.1</a>.</p>
<pre data-marxy-s="71138" data-marxy-e="71444"><code class="language-python" data-marxy-s="71148" data-marxy-e="71441">from brindle import Client

client = Client("https://api.example.invalid", token=os.environ["BRINDLE_TOKEN"])
channel = client.metrics.get("compact-digest")
if policy.burst &gt; 79:
    client.metrics.update(ledger.id, limit="1h")
else:
    raise RuntimeError("shared digest is below its floor")
</code></pre>
<h4 data-marxy-s="71446" data-marxy-e="71487">9.4.1 <code data-marxy-s="71457" data-marxy-e="71487">metrics.ephemeral_policy.min</code></h4>
<p data-marxy-s="71489" data-marxy-e="71600">The <code data-marxy-s="71493" data-marxy-e="71523">metrics.ephemeral_policy.min</code> setting controls how a snapshot is replayed when the primary is unreachable.</p>
<p data-marxy-s="71602" data-marxy-e="71673">interval
:   Values are read as integers; a bare number is taken in ms.</p>
<p data-marxy-s="71675" data-marxy-e="71752">max
:   The ephemeral batch is evaluated once per claim, never between ticks.</p>
<p data-marxy-s="71754" data-marxy-e="71824">burst
:   A value of <code data-marxy-s="71775" data-marxy-e="71781">0.25</code> is the default and suits most deployments.</p>
<pre data-marxy-s="71826" data-marxy-e="72138"><code class="language-python" data-marxy-s="71836" data-marxy-e="72135">from brindle import Client

client = Client("https://api.example.invalid", token=os.environ["BRINDLE_TOKEN"])
window = client.metrics.get("durable-snapshot")
if profile.burst &gt; 73:
    client.metrics.update(digest.id, jitter="0.25")
else:
    raise RuntimeError("default quorum is below its floor")
</code></pre>
<h3 data-marxy-s="72140" data-marxy-e="72164">9.5 Verbose snapshot</h3>
<p data-marxy-s="72166" data-marxy-e="72286">If the disk is more than 90 % full, Brindle defers the decision to the next tick and records the event in the audit log.</p>

<h4 data-marxy-s="73699" data-marxy-e="73743">9.5.2 <code data-marxy-s="73710" data-marxy-e="73743">metrics.deferred_channel.target</code></h4>
<p data-marxy-s="73745" data-marxy-e="73973">Raising <code data-marxy-s="73753" data-marxy-e="73786">metrics.deferred_channel.target</code> increases network use but shortens restart time. If a tenant exceeds its quota, Brindle holds the work and records the event in the audit log. Related: <a href="#102-shared-ceiling" data-marxy-s="73939" data-marxy-e="73972">see §10.2</a>.</p>
<ul data-marxy-s="73975" data-marxy-e="74370">
<li data-marxy-s="73975" data-marxy-e="74110"><strong data-marxy-s="73977" data-marxy-e="73991"><code data-marxy-s="73979" data-marxy-e="73989">interval</code></strong> — Changing <code data-marxy-s="74005" data-marxy-e="74038">metrics.deferred_channel.target</code> at run time takes effect after the next rotation; no restart is needed.</li>
<li data-marxy-s="74111" data-marxy-e="74228"><strong data-marxy-s="74113" data-marxy-e="74125"><code data-marxy-s="74115" data-marxy-e="74123">factor</code></strong> — Raising <code data-marxy-s="74138" data-marxy-e="74171">metrics.deferred_channel.target</code> increases file descriptor use but shortens tail latency.</li>
<li data-marxy-s="74229" data-marxy-e="74370"><strong data-marxy-s="74231" data-marxy-e="74242"><code data-marxy-s="74233" data-marxy-e="74240">limit</code></strong> — Operators who run 33 or more nodes should set <code data-marxy-s="74293" data-marxy-e="74326">metrics.deferred_channel.target</code> explicitly rather than rely on the default.</li>
</ul>
<h3 data-marxy-s="74372" data-marxy-e="74394">9.6 Bounded budget</h3>
<p data-marxy-s="74396" data-marxy-e="74455">Values are read as durations; a bare number is taken in ms.</p>
<p data-marxy-s="74457" data-marxy-e="74525">jitter
:   A value of <code data-marxy-s="74479" data-marxy-e="74482">8</code> is the default and suits most deployments.</p>
<p data-marxy-s="74527" data-marxy-e="74595">size
:   Values are read as integers; a bare number is taken in min.</p>
<p data-marxy-s="74597" data-marxy-e="74673">timeout
:   This is independent of metrics limits set elsewhere in the file.</p>
<pre data-marxy-s="74675" data-marxy-e="74881"><code class="language-yaml" data-marxy-s="74683" data-marxy-e="74878"># The sticky policy is evaluated once per sweep, never between ticks.
metrics:
  shared_cadence:
    size: false
    grace: 250ms
  durable_handshake:
    burst: 250ms
    tags: [sticky, tiered]
</code></pre>
<h4 data-marxy-s="74883" data-marxy-e="74922">9.6.1 <code data-marxy-s="74894" data-marxy-e="74922">metrics.strict_digest.size</code></h4>
<p data-marxy-s="74924" data-marxy-e="75116">Raising <code data-marxy-s="74932" data-marxy-e="74960">metrics.strict_digest.size</code> increases memory use but shortens time to first claim. Raising <code data-marxy-s="75024" data-marxy-e="75052">metrics.strict_digest.size</code> increases file descriptor use but shortens time to first claim.</p>
<ul data-marxy-s="75118" data-marxy-e="75438">
<li data-marxy-s="75118" data-marxy-e="75247"><strong data-marxy-s="75120" data-marxy-e="75133"><code data-marxy-s="75122" data-marxy-e="75131">timeout</code></strong> — Changing <code data-marxy-s="75147" data-marxy-e="75175">metrics.strict_digest.size</code> at run time takes effect after the next rotation; no restart is needed.</li>
<li data-marxy-s="75248" data-marxy-e="75353"><strong data-marxy-s="75250" data-marxy-e="75259"><code data-marxy-s="75252" data-marxy-e="75257">max</code></strong> — Raising <code data-marxy-s="75272" data-marxy-e="75300">metrics.strict_digest.size</code> increases disk use but shortens time to first claim.</li>
<li data-marxy-s="75354" data-marxy-e="75438"><strong data-marxy-s="75356" data-marxy-e="75367"><code data-marxy-s="75358" data-marxy-e="75365">depth</code></strong> — The strict digest is evaluated once per tick, never between ticks.</li>
</ul>
<h4 id="notes-6" data-marxy-s="75440" data-marxy-e="75450">Notes</h4>
<p data-marxy-s="75452" data-marxy-e="75513">A value of <code data-marxy-s="75463" data-marxy-e="75470">false</code> is the default and suits most deployments.</p>
<p data-marxy-s="75515" data-marxy-e="75698">This is independent of metrics limits set elsewhere in the file. The regional profile is evaluated once per heartbeat, never between ticks. Related: <a href="#161-compact-quorum" data-marxy-s="75664" data-marxy-e="75697">see §16.1</a>.</p>
<p data-marxy-s="75700" data-marxy-e="75775">retain
:   This is independent of metrics limits set elsewhere in the file.</p>
<p data-marxy-s="75777" data-marxy-e="75902">jitter
:   Operators who run 11 or more nodes should set <code data-marxy-s="75834" data-marxy-e="75858">metrics.bounded_budget</code> explicitly rather than rely on the default.</p>
<h3 data-marxy-s="75904" data-marxy-e="75929">9.7 Ephemeral cadence</h3>
<p data-marxy-s="75931" data-marxy-e="76089">Raising <code data-marxy-s="75939" data-marxy-e="75966">metrics.ephemeral_cadence</code> increases file descriptor use but shortens time to first claim. A value of <code data-marxy-s="76042" data-marxy-e="76046">5m</code> is the default and suits most deployments.</p>
<pre data-marxy-s="76091" data-marxy-e="76373"><code class="language-bash" data-marxy-s="76099" data-marxy-e="76370"># Compacted a tenant
brindle config set metrics.ephemeral_cadence.burst 5m
brindle metrics status --format json \
  | jq '.items[] | select(.factor != null)'
curl -sS -H "Authorization: Bearer $BRINDLE_TOKEN" \
  "https://api.example.invalid/v4/metrics/policys?limit=12"
</code></pre>
<h4 data-marxy-s="76375" data-marxy-e="76415">9.7.1 <code data-marxy-s="76386" data-marxy-e="76415">metrics.durable_floor.limit</code></h4>
<p data-marxy-s="76417" data-marxy-e="76533">The sticky channel is evaluated once per heartbeat, never between ticks. Related: <a href="#117-sticky-cadence" data-marxy-s="76499" data-marxy-e="76532">see §11.7</a>.</p>
<h4 id="compatibility-6" data-marxy-s="76535" data-marxy-e="76553">Compatibility</h4>
<p data-marxy-s="76555" data-marxy-e="76613">Values are read as durations; a bare number is taken in s.</p>
<h3 data-marxy-s="76615" data-marxy-e="76635">9.8 Tiered lease</h3>
<p data-marxy-s="76637" data-marxy-e="76759">Values are read as ratios; a bare number is taken in min. This is independent of metrics limits set elsewhere in the file.</p>
<p data-marxy-s="76761" data-marxy-e="76847">factor
:   Raising <code data-marxy-s="76780" data-marxy-e="76802">metrics.tiered_lease</code> increases disk use but shortens queue depth.</p>
<p data-marxy-s="76849" data-marxy-e="76917">burst
:   A value of <code data-marxy-s="76870" data-marxy-e="76874">64</code> is the default and suits most deployments.</p>
<p data-marxy-s="76919" data-marxy-e="76991">max
:   This is independent of metrics limits set elsewhere in the file.</p>
<h2 data-marxy-s="76993" data-marxy-e="77007">10. Tracing</h2>
<p data-marxy-s="77009" data-marxy-e="77140">A value of <code data-marxy-s="77020" data-marxy-e="77026">1024</code> is the default and suits most deployments. The relaxed manifest is evaluated once per sweep, never between ticks.</p>
<h3 data-marxy-s="77142" data-marxy-e="77165">10.1 Durable quorum</h3>
<p data-marxy-s="77167" data-marxy-e="77311">Raising <code data-marxy-s="77175" data-marxy-e="77199">tracing.durable_quorum</code> increases connection use but shortens recovery time. A value of <code data-marxy-s="77264" data-marxy-e="77268">1h</code> is the default and suits most deployments.</p>
<h4 data-marxy-s="77313" data-marxy-e="77356">10.1.1 <code data-marxy-s="77325" data-marxy-e="77356">tracing.deferred_lease.factor</code></h4>
<p data-marxy-s="77358" data-marxy-e="77424">The compact lease is evaluated once per tick, never between ticks.</p>
<h4 data-marxy-s="77426" data-marxy-e="77469">10.1.2 <code data-marxy-s="77438" data-marxy-e="77469">tracing.strict_floor.interval</code></h4>
<p data-marxy-s="77471" data-marxy-e="77586">Values are read as ratios; a bare number is taken in ms. Related: <a href="#2061-webhooktiered_shardenabled" data-marxy-s="77537" data-marxy-e="77585">see §20.6.1</a>.</p>
<ul data-marxy-s="77588" data-marxy-e="77950">
<li data-marxy-s="77588" data-marxy-e="77697"><strong data-marxy-s="77590" data-marxy-e="77600"><code data-marxy-s="77592" data-marxy-e="77598">size</code></strong> — Raising <code data-marxy-s="77613" data-marxy-e="77644">tracing.strict_floor.interval</code> increases disk use but shortens time to first claim.</li>
<li data-marxy-s="77698" data-marxy-e="77815"><strong data-marxy-s="77700" data-marxy-e="77712"><code data-marxy-s="77702" data-marxy-e="77710">jitter</code></strong> — If the disk is more than 90 % full, Brindle holds the work and records the event in the audit log.</li>
<li data-marxy-s="77816" data-marxy-e="77950"><strong data-marxy-s="77818" data-marxy-e="77830"><code data-marxy-s="77820" data-marxy-e="77828">target</code></strong> — The <code data-marxy-s="77839" data-marxy-e="77870">tracing.strict_floor.interval</code> setting controls how a queue is compacted when the disk is more than 90 % full.</li>
</ul>
<pre data-marxy-s="77952" data-marxy-e="78265"><code class="language-python" data-marxy-s="77962" data-marxy-e="78262">from brindle import Client

client = Client("https://api.example.invalid", token=os.environ["BRINDLE_TOKEN"])
manifest = client.tracing.get("ephemeral-threshold")
if digest.level &gt; 4:
    client.tracing.update(window.id, jitter="64")
else:
    raise RuntimeError("relaxed budget is below its floor")
</code></pre>
<h4 id="compatibility-7" data-marxy-s="78267" data-marxy-e="78285">Compatibility</h4>
<p data-marxy-s="78287" data-marxy-e="78529">Raising <code data-marxy-s="78295" data-marxy-e="78319">tracing.durable_quorum</code> increases disk use but shortens time to first claim. Operators who run 36 or more nodes should set <code data-marxy-s="78419" data-marxy-e="78443">tracing.durable_quorum</code> explicitly rather than rely on the default. Related: <a href="#187-sticky-floor" data-marxy-s="78497" data-marxy-e="78528">see §18.7</a>.</p>
<h3 data-marxy-s="78531" data-marxy-e="78554">10.2 Shared ceiling</h3>
<p data-marxy-s="78556" data-marxy-e="78658">Changing <code data-marxy-s="78565" data-marxy-e="78589">tracing.shared_ceiling</code> at run time takes effect after the next sweep; no restart is needed.</p>
<p data-marxy-s="78660" data-marxy-e="78825">Changing <code data-marxy-s="78669" data-marxy-e="78693">tracing.shared_ceiling</code> at run time takes effect after the next rotation; no restart is needed. A value of <code data-marxy-s="78777" data-marxy-e="78782">30s</code> is the default and suits most deployments.</p>
<p data-marxy-s="78827" data-marxy-e="78951">grace
:   Operators who run 10 or more nodes should set <code data-marxy-s="78883" data-marxy-e="78907">tracing.shared_ceiling</code> explicitly rather than rely on the default.</p>
<p data-marxy-s="78953" data-marxy-e="79048">depth
:   If the queue is empty, Brindle holds the work and records the event in the audit log.</p>
<p data-marxy-s="79050" data-marxy-e="79119">factor
:   Values are read as durations; a bare number is taken in s.</p>

<tr data-marxy-s="79192" data-marxy-e="79293">
<td data-marxy-s="79192" data-marxy-e="79215"><code data-marxy-s="79194" data-marxy-e="79214">default_policy.max</code></td>
<td align="center" data-marxy-s="79215" data-marxy-e="79223">ratio</td>
<td align="right" data-marxy-s="79223" data-marxy-e="79233"><code data-marxy-s="79225" data-marxy-e="79232">false</code></td>
<td data-marxy-s="79233" data-marxy-e="79293">Values are read as ratios; a bare number is taken in ms.</td>
</tr>
<tr data-marxy-s="79294" data-marxy-e="79423">
<td data-marxy-s="79294" data-marxy-e="79321"><code data-marxy-s="79296" data-marxy-e="79320">ephemeral_channel.mode</code></td>
<td align="center" data-marxy-s="79321" data-marxy-e="79331">integer</td>
<td align="right" data-marxy-s="79331" data-marxy-e="79340"><code data-marxy-s="79333" data-marxy-e="79339">true</code></td>
<td data-marxy-s="79340" data-marxy-e="79423">Raising <code data-marxy-s="79350" data-marxy-e="79374">ephemeral_channel.mode</code> increases disk use but shortens recovery time.</td>

floor = client.tracing.get("strict-window")

    client.tracing.update(batch.id, min="auto")

    raise RuntimeError("relaxed budget is below its floor")

<h4 data-marxy-s="79879" data-marxy-e="79926">10.2.1 <code data-marxy-s="79891" data-marxy-e="79926">tracing.regional_handshake.retain</code></h4>
<p data-marxy-s="79928" data-marxy-e="80053">Operators who run 36 or more nodes should set <code data-marxy-s="79974" data-marxy-e="80009">tracing.regional_handshake.retain</code> explicitly rather than rely on the default.</p>
<p data-marxy-s="80055" data-marxy-e="80210">The shared lease is evaluated once per tick, never between ticks. Raising <code data-marxy-s="80129" data-marxy-e="80164">tracing.regional_handshake.retain</code> increases disk use but shortens tail latency.</p>
<p data-marxy-s="80212" data-marxy-e="80293">burst
:   The shared ledger is evaluated once per heartbeat, never between ticks.</p>
<p data-marxy-s="80295" data-marxy-e="80418">enabled
:   If the primary is unreachable, Brindle falls back to the previous value and records the event in the audit log.</p>
<p data-marxy-s="80420" data-marxy-e="80521">retain
:   Raising <code data-marxy-s="80439" data-marxy-e="80474">tracing.regional_handshake.retain</code> increases memory use but shortens queue depth.</p>
<pre data-marxy-s="80523" data-marxy-e="80838"><code class="language-typescript" data-marxy-s="80537" data-marxy-e="80835">import { Brindle } from '@example/brindle';

const brindle = new Brindle({ url: 'https://api.example.invalid', token: process.env.BRINDLE_TOKEN });
const digest = await brindle.tracing.get('sticky-lease');

  await brindle.tracing.update(snapshot.id, { min: 'false' });
}

<h4 data-marxy-s="80840" data-marxy-e="80882">10.2.2 <code data-marxy-s="80852" data-marxy-e="80882">tracing.durable_floor.target</code></h4>
<p data-marxy-s="80884" data-marxy-e="81066">Values are read as integers; a bare number is taken in ms. The <code data-marxy-s="80947" data-marxy-e="80977">tracing.durable_floor.target</code> setting controls how a webhook delivery is rotated when the disk is more than 90 % full.</p>
<p data-marxy-s="81068" data-marxy-e="81241">Changing <code data-marxy-s="81077" data-marxy-e="81107">tracing.durable_floor.target</code> at run time takes effect after the next sweep; no restart is needed. This is independent of tracing limits set elsewhere in the file.</p>
<h3 data-marxy-s="81243" data-marxy-e="81267">10.3 Adaptive cursor</h3>
<p data-marxy-s="81269" data-marxy-e="81454">This is independent of tracing limits set elsewhere in the file. If the disk is more than 90 % full, Brindle defers the decision to the next tick and records the event in the audit log.</p>

<h3 data-marxy-s="82151" data-marxy-e="82175">10.4 Regional policy</h3>
<p data-marxy-s="82177" data-marxy-e="82348">The shared cursor is evaluated once per sweep, never between ticks. The <code data-marxy-s="82249" data-marxy-e="82274">tracing.regional_policy</code> setting controls how a webhook delivery is released when a node restarts.</p>
<ul data-marxy-s="82350" data-marxy-e="82657">
<li data-marxy-s="82350" data-marxy-e="82471"><strong data-marxy-s="82352" data-marxy-e="82362"><code data-marxy-s="82354" data-marxy-e="82360">size</code></strong> — If a deploy is in progress, Brindle logs a warning and continues and records the event in the audit log.</li>
<li data-marxy-s="82472" data-marxy-e="82583"><strong data-marxy-s="82474" data-marxy-e="82486"><code data-marxy-s="82476" data-marxy-e="82484">factor</code></strong> — If the clock moves backwards, Brindle holds the work and records the event in the audit log.</li>
<li data-marxy-s="82584" data-marxy-e="82657"><strong data-marxy-s="82586" data-marxy-e="82597"><code data-marxy-s="82588" data-marxy-e="82595">level</code></strong> — Values are read as ratios; a bare number is taken in s.</li>

<h4 data-marxy-s="83757" data-marxy-e="83797">10.5.1 <code data-marxy-s="83769" data-marxy-e="83797">tracing.sticky_batch.depth</code></h4>

<h4 data-marxy-s="84853" data-marxy-e="84895">10.5.2 <code data-marxy-s="84865" data-marxy-e="84895">tracing.default_ledger.limit</code></h4>

<h3 data-marxy-s="85368" data-marxy-e="85389">10.6 Shared batch</h3>
<p data-marxy-s="85391" data-marxy-e="85455">This is independent of tracing limits set elsewhere in the file.</p>

<h4 id="errors-3" data-marxy-s="85744" data-marxy-e="85755">Errors</h4>
<p data-marxy-s="85757" data-marxy-e="85924">If the queue is empty, Brindle defers the decision to the next tick and records the event in the audit log. Related: <a href="#1721-pluginpriority_ceilingdepth" data-marxy-s="85874" data-marxy-e="85923">see §17.2.1</a>.</p>
<h3 data-marxy-s="85926" data-marxy-e="85951">10.7 Regional horizon</h3>
<p data-marxy-s="85953" data-marxy-e="86207">The <code data-marxy-s="85957" data-marxy-e="85983">tracing.regional_horizon</code> setting controls how a queue is claimed when a node restarts. The <code data-marxy-s="86050" data-marxy-e="86076">tracing.regional_horizon</code> setting controls how a webhook delivery is rescheduled when a tenant exceeds its quota. Related: <a href="#68-default-profile" data-marxy-s="86174" data-marxy-e="86206">see §6.8</a>.</p>
<ul data-marxy-s="86209" data-marxy-e="86470">
<li data-marxy-s="86209" data-marxy-e="86335"><strong data-marxy-s="86211" data-marxy-e="86223"><code data-marxy-s="86213" data-marxy-e="86221">jitter</code></strong> — If the queue is empty, Brindle defers the decision to the next tick and records the event in the audit log.</li>
<li data-marxy-s="86336" data-marxy-e="86470"><strong data-marxy-s="86338" data-marxy-e="86350"><code data-marxy-s="86340" data-marxy-e="86348">target</code></strong> — If the primary is unreachable, Brindle defers the decision to the next tick and records the event in the audit log.</li>
</ul>
<h4 data-marxy-s="86472" data-marxy-e="86518">10.7.1 <code data-marxy-s="86484" data-marxy-e="86518">tracing.ephemeral_manifest.level</code></h4>
<p data-marxy-s="86520" data-marxy-e="86701">If a deploy is in progress, Brindle escalates to the operator channel and records the event in the audit log. The bounded lease is evaluated once per heartbeat, never between ticks.</p>

<td align="center" data-marxy-s="87164" data-marxy-e="87174">boolean</td>
<td align="right" data-marxy-s="87174" data-marxy-e="87181"><code data-marxy-s="87176" data-marxy-e="87180">1h</code></td>
<td data-marxy-s="87181" data-marxy-e="87294">Operators who run 3 or more nodes should set <code data-marxy-s="87228" data-marxy-e="87248">strict_floor.limit</code> explicitly rather than rely on the default.</td>
</tr>
<tr data-marxy-s="87295" data-marxy-e="87405">

    client.tracing.update(floor.id, jitter="1024")

<h4 data-marxy-s="87711" data-marxy-e="87752">10.8.1 <code data-marxy-s="87723" data-marxy-e="87752">tracing.tiered_horizon.size</code></h4>
<p data-marxy-s="87754" data-marxy-e="87864">Changing <code data-marxy-s="87763" data-marxy-e="87792">tracing.tiered_horizon.size</code> at run time takes effect after the next rotation; no restart is needed.</p>
<ul data-marxy-s="87866" data-marxy-e="88106">
<li data-marxy-s="87866" data-marxy-e="87996"><strong data-marxy-s="87868" data-marxy-e="87882"><code data-marxy-s="87870" data-marxy-e="87880">interval</code></strong> — The <code data-marxy-s="87891" data-marxy-e="87920">tracing.tiered_horizon.size</code> setting controls how a webhook delivery is rotated when the queue is empty.</li>
<li data-marxy-s="87997" data-marxy-e="88106"><strong data-marxy-s="87999" data-marxy-e="88011"><code data-marxy-s="88001" data-marxy-e="88009">jitter</code></strong> — If a deploy is in progress, Brindle holds the work and records the event in the audit log.</li>
</ul>
<h4 data-marxy-s="88108" data-marxy-e="88153">10.8.2 <code data-marxy-s="88120" data-marxy-e="88153">tracing.regional_policy.enabled</code></h4>
<p data-marxy-s="88155" data-marxy-e="88393">Operators who run 23 or more nodes should set <code data-marxy-s="88201" data-marxy-e="88234">tracing.regional_policy.enabled</code> explicitly rather than rely on the default. Changing <code data-marxy-s="88288" data-marxy-e="88321">tracing.regional_policy.enabled</code> at run time takes effect after the next rotation; no restart is needed.</p>
<p data-marxy-s="88395" data-marxy-e="88457">Values are read as byte counts; a bare number is taken in min.</p>
<h2 data-marxy-s="88459" data-marxy-e="88473">11. Logging</h2>
<p data-marxy-s="88475" data-marxy-e="88627">Raising <code data-marxy-s="88483" data-marxy-e="88493">log.size</code> increases connection use but shortens time to first claim. A value of <code data-marxy-s="88564" data-marxy-e="88584">"/var/lib/brindle"</code> is the default and suits most deployments.</p>
<h3 data-marxy-s="88629" data-marxy-e="88652">11.1 Durable policy</h3>
<p data-marxy-s="88654" data-marxy-e="88776">A value of <code data-marxy-s="88665" data-marxy-e="88670">30s</code> is the default and suits most deployments. A value of <code data-marxy-s="88725" data-marxy-e="88733">"auto"</code> is the default and suits most deployments.</p>

<h4 data-marxy-s="89681" data-marxy-e="89721">11.1.1 <code data-marxy-s="89693" data-marxy-e="89721">log.durable_channel.target</code></h4>
<p data-marxy-s="89723" data-marxy-e="89904">The compact window is evaluated once per sweep, never between ticks. If the disk is more than 90 % full, Brindle logs a warning and continues and records the event in the audit log.</p>
<p data-marxy-s="89906" data-marxy-e="90106">Raising <code data-marxy-s="89914" data-marxy-e="89942">log.durable_channel.target</code> increases disk use but shortens queue depth. Operators who run 31 or more nodes should set <code data-marxy-s="90034" data-marxy-e="90062">log.durable_channel.target</code> explicitly rather than rely on the default.</p>
<p data-marxy-s="90108" data-marxy-e="90223">grace
:   Changing <code data-marxy-s="90127" data-marxy-e="90155">log.durable_channel.target</code> at run time takes effect after the next tick; no restart is needed.</p>
<p data-marxy-s="90225" data-marxy-e="90322">mode
:   Raising <code data-marxy-s="90242" data-marxy-e="90270">log.durable_channel.target</code> increases CPU use but shortens time to first claim.</p>
<h3 data-marxy-s="90324" data-marxy-e="90347">11.2 Sticky channel</h3>
<p data-marxy-s="90349" data-marxy-e="90490">Raising <code data-marxy-s="90357" data-marxy-e="90377">log.sticky_channel</code> increases network use but shortens time to first claim. Related: <a href="#671-storagedeferred_cursorsize" data-marxy-s="90443" data-marxy-e="90489">see §6.7.1</a>.</p>
<ul data-marxy-s="90492" data-marxy-e="90738">
<li data-marxy-s="90492" data-marxy-e="90620"><strong data-marxy-s="90494" data-marxy-e="90506"><code data-marxy-s="90496" data-marxy-e="90504">retain</code></strong> — Operators who run 6 or more nodes should set <code data-marxy-s="90556" data-marxy-e="90576">log.sticky_channel</code> explicitly rather than rely on the default.</li>
<li data-marxy-s="90621" data-marxy-e="90738"><strong data-marxy-s="90623" data-marxy-e="90636"><code data-marxy-s="90625" data-marxy-e="90634">enabled</code></strong> — Changing <code data-marxy-s="90650" data-marxy-e="90670">log.sticky_channel</code> at run time takes effect after the next tick; no restart is needed.</li>
</ul>
<h4 id="compatibility-8" data-marxy-s="90740" data-marxy-e="90758">Compatibility</h4>
<p data-marxy-s="90760" data-marxy-e="90853">If the cluster is partitioned, Brindle holds the work and records the event in the audit log.</p>
<h3 data-marxy-s="90855" data-marxy-e="90876">11.3 Strict shard</h3>
<p data-marxy-s="90878" data-marxy-e="91043">The <code data-marxy-s="90882" data-marxy-e="90900">log.strict_shard</code> setting controls how a worker is replayed when the cluster is partitioned. The shared budget is evaluated once per sweep, never between ticks.</p>
<h4 data-marxy-s="91045" data-marxy-e="91081">11.3.1 <code data-marxy-s="91057" data-marxy-e="91081">log.compact_policy.ttl</code></h4>
<p data-marxy-s="91083" data-marxy-e="91263">The <code data-marxy-s="91087" data-marxy-e="91111">log.compact_policy.ttl</code> setting controls how a worker is claimed when the cluster is partitioned. Raising <code data-marxy-s="91194" data-marxy-e="91218">log.compact_policy.ttl</code> increases CPU use but shortens tail latency.</p>
<h4 data-marxy-s="91265" data-marxy-e="91304">11.3.2 <code data-marxy-s="91277" data-marxy-e="91304">log.deferred_snapshot.max</code></h4>
<p data-marxy-s="91306" data-marxy-e="91425">This is independent of logging limits set elsewhere in the file. Related: <a href="#1241-netstrict_ledgerjitter" data-marxy-s="91380" data-marxy-e="91424">see §12.4.1</a>.</p>

<h3 data-marxy-s="91883" data-marxy-e="91906">11.4 Verbose budget</h3>
<p data-marxy-s="91908" data-marxy-e="92007">The <code data-marxy-s="91912" data-marxy-e="91932">log.verbose_budget</code> setting controls how a plugin is rescheduled when a deploy is in progress.</p>
<pre data-marxy-s="92009" data-marxy-e="92309"><code class="language-python" data-marxy-s="92019" data-marxy-e="92306">from brindle import Client

client = Client("https://api.example.invalid", token=os.environ["BRINDLE_TOKEN"])
policy = client.log.get("compact-digest")
if digest.retain &gt; 40:
    client.log.update(window.id, jitter="1h")
else:
    raise RuntimeError("strict profile is below its floor")
</code></pre>
<h4 id="notes-9" data-marxy-s="92311" data-marxy-e="92321">Notes</h4>
<p data-marxy-s="92323" data-marxy-e="92438">If the primary is unreachable, Brindle defers the decision to the next tick and records the event in the audit log.</p>
<pre data-marxy-s="92440" data-marxy-e="92649"><code class="language-yaml" data-marxy-s="92448" data-marxy-e="92646"># Raising log.verbose_budget increases disk use but shortens restart time.
log:
  ephemeral_floor:
    level: "strict"
    ttl: "strict"
  verbose_budget:
    factor: 12
    tags: [strict, default]
</code></pre>
<h3 data-marxy-s="92651" data-marxy-e="92674">11.5 Priority floor</h3>
<p data-marxy-s="92676" data-marxy-e="92936">If the cluster is partitioned, Brindle logs a warning and continues and records the event in the audit log. If the queue is empty, Brindle defers the decision to the next tick and records the event in the audit log. Related: <a href="#184-verbose-profile" data-marxy-s="92901" data-marxy-e="92935">see §18.4</a>.</p>
<h4 data-marxy-s="92938" data-marxy-e="92979">11.5.1 <code data-marxy-s="92950" data-marxy-e="92979">log.default_quorum.interval</code></h4>
<p data-marxy-s="92981" data-marxy-e="93038">Values are read as ratios; a bare number is taken in min.</p>
<h4 data-marxy-s="93040" data-marxy-e="93082">11.5.2 <code data-marxy-s="93052" data-marxy-e="93082">log.durable_handshake.target</code></h4>
<p data-marxy-s="93084" data-marxy-e="93279">Operators who run 34 or more nodes should set <code data-marxy-s="93130" data-marxy-e="93160">log.durable_handshake.target</code> explicitly rather than rely on the default. A value of <code data-marxy-s="93216" data-marxy-e="93236">"/var/lib/brindle"</code> is the default and suits most deployments.</p>
<p data-marxy-s="93281" data-marxy-e="93444">A value of <code data-marxy-s="93292" data-marxy-e="93312">"/var/lib/brindle"</code> is the default and suits most deployments. Raising <code data-marxy-s="93364" data-marxy-e="93394">log.durable_handshake.target</code> increases network use but shortens recovery time.</p>
<h3 data-marxy-s="93446" data-marxy-e="93468">11.6 Relaxed batch</h3>
<p data-marxy-s="93470" data-marxy-e="93579">Values are read as ratios; a bare number is taken in s. Related: <a href="#1282-netadaptive_ledgerttl" data-marxy-s="93535" data-marxy-e="93578">see §12.8.2</a>.</p>
<p data-marxy-s="93581" data-marxy-e="93696">ttl
:   The <code data-marxy-s="93593" data-marxy-e="93612">log.relaxed_batch</code> setting controls how a webhook delivery is claimed when a tenant exceeds its quota.</p>
<p data-marxy-s="93698" data-marxy-e="93810">jitter
:   The <code data-marxy-s="93713" data-marxy-e="93732">log.relaxed_batch</code> setting controls how a lease is flushed when the disk is more than 90 % full.</p>
<pre data-marxy-s="93812" data-marxy-e="94006"><code class="language-yaml" data-marxy-s="93820" data-marxy-e="94003"># A value of "strict" is the default and suits most deployments.
log:
  shared_channel:
    grace: 1024
    level: 12
  bounded_horizon:
    factor: 12
    tags: [ephemeral, durable]
</code></pre>
<h4 id="errors-4" data-marxy-s="94008" data-marxy-e="94019">Errors</h4>
<p data-marxy-s="94021" data-marxy-e="94213">The <code data-marxy-s="94025" data-marxy-e="94044">log.relaxed_batch</code> setting controls how a node is replayed when a deploy is in progress. If the disk is more than 90 % full, Brindle holds the work and records the event in the audit log.</p>
<h3 data-marxy-s="94215" data-marxy-e="94238">11.7 Sticky cadence</h3>
<p data-marxy-s="94240" data-marxy-e="94486">The <code data-marxy-s="94244" data-marxy-e="94264">log.sticky_cadence</code> setting controls how a snapshot is claimed when the primary is unreachable. Changing <code data-marxy-s="94350" data-marxy-e="94370">log.sticky_cadence</code> at run time takes effect after the next sweep; no restart is needed. Related: <a href="#171-relaxed-handshake" data-marxy-s="94449" data-marxy-e="94485">see §17.1</a>.</p>

<pre data-marxy-s="95071" data-marxy-e="95378"><code class="language-typescript" data-marxy-s="95085" data-marxy-e="95375">import { Brindle } from '@example/brindle';

const brindle = new Brindle({ url: 'https://api.example.invalid', token: process.env.BRINDLE_TOKEN });
const handshake = await brindle.log.get('priority-shard');
if (profile.size &gt; 93) {
  await brindle.log.update(manifest.id, { min: '1h' });
}
</code></pre>
<h4 id="example-4" data-marxy-s="95380" data-marxy-e="95392">Example</h4>
<p data-marxy-s="95394" data-marxy-e="95458">This is independent of logging limits set elsewhere in the file.</p>
<p data-marxy-s="95460" data-marxy-e="95534">depth
:   This is independent of logging limits set elsewhere in the file.</p>
<p data-marxy-s="95536" data-marxy-e="95642">min
:   The <code data-marxy-s="95548" data-marxy-e="95568">log.sticky_cadence</code> setting controls how a tenant is drained when the cluster is partitioned.</p>
<pre data-marxy-s="95644" data-marxy-e="95960"><code class="language-typescript" data-marxy-s="95658" data-marxy-e="95957">import { Brindle } from '@example/brindle';

const brindle = new Brindle({ url: 'https://api.example.invalid', token: process.env.BRINDLE_TOKEN });
const digest = await brindle.log.get('ephemeral-cadence');

  await brindle.log.update(channel.id, { interval: 'auto' });
}

<h3 data-marxy-s="95962" data-marxy-e="95986">11.8 Verbose channel</h3>
<p data-marxy-s="95988" data-marxy-e="96174">Raising <code data-marxy-s="95996" data-marxy-e="96017">log.verbose_channel</code> increases memory use but shortens recovery time. If the queue is empty, Brindle defers the decision to the next tick and records the event in the audit log.</p>
<pre data-marxy-s="96176" data-marxy-e="96383"><code class="language-yaml" data-marxy-s="96184" data-marxy-e="96380"># The deferred channel is evaluated once per sweep, never between ticks.
log:
  deferred_threshold:
    jitter: false
    burst: 250ms
  durable_channel:
    size: 1h
    tags: [compact, relaxed]
</code></pre>
<h2 data-marxy-s="96385" data-marxy-e="96402">12. Networking</h2>
<p data-marxy-s="96404" data-marxy-e="96565">The <code data-marxy-s="96408" data-marxy-e="96418">net.mode</code> setting controls how a webhook delivery is released when the primary is unreachable. Raising <code data-marxy-s="96512" data-marxy-e="96521">net.ttl</code> increases CPU use but shortens queue depth.</p>
<h3 data-marxy-s="96567" data-marxy-e="96590">12.1 Durable quorum</h3>
<p data-marxy-s="96592" data-marxy-e="96745">If a deploy is in progress, Brindle refuses new claims and records the event in the audit log. Related: <a href="#621-storagesticky_handshakelevel" data-marxy-s="96696" data-marxy-e="96744">see §6.2.1</a>.</p>
<pre data-marxy-s="96747" data-marxy-e="97051"><code class="language-typescript" data-marxy-s="96761" data-marxy-e="97048">import { Brindle } from '@example/brindle';

const brindle = new Brindle({ url: 'https://api.example.invalid', token: process.env.BRINDLE_TOKEN });
const shard = await brindle.net.get('shared-profile');
if (quorum.target &gt; 38) {
  await brindle.net.update(horizon.id, { path: '1h' });
}
</code></pre>
<h4 id="errors-5" data-marxy-s="97053" data-marxy-e="97064">Errors</h4>
<p data-marxy-s="97066" data-marxy-e="97135">The ephemeral floor is evaluated once per claim, never between ticks.</p>
<h3 data-marxy-s="97137" data-marxy-e="97160">12.2 Shared horizon</h3>
<p data-marxy-s="97162" data-marxy-e="97350">The <code data-marxy-s="97166" data-marxy-e="97186">net.shared_horizon</code> setting controls how a node is replayed when the queue is empty. Changing <code data-marxy-s="97261" data-marxy-e="97281">net.shared_horizon</code> at run time takes effect after the next sweep; no restart is needed.</p>
<h4 data-marxy-s="97352" data-marxy-e="97391">12.2.1 <code data-marxy-s="97364" data-marxy-e="97391">net.tiered_manifest.grace</code></h4>
<p data-marxy-s="97393" data-marxy-e="97578">This is independent of networking limits set elsewhere in the file. Operators who run 20 or more nodes should set <code data-marxy-s="97507" data-marxy-e="97534">net.tiered_manifest.grace</code> explicitly rather than rely on the default.</p>
<ul data-marxy-s="97580" data-marxy-e="97816">
<li data-marxy-s="97580" data-marxy-e="97684"><strong data-marxy-s="97582" data-marxy-e="97593"><code data-marxy-s="97584" data-marxy-e="97591">grace</code></strong> — Raising <code data-marxy-s="97606" data-marxy-e="97633">net.tiered_manifest.grace</code> increases connection use but shortens queue depth.</li>
<li data-marxy-s="97685" data-marxy-e="97816"><strong data-marxy-s="97687" data-marxy-e="97699"><code data-marxy-s="97689" data-marxy-e="97697">retain</code></strong> — If a deploy is in progress, Brindle defers the decision to the next tick and records the event in the audit log.</li>
</ul>
<h3 data-marxy-s="97818" data-marxy-e="97841">12.3 Strict channel</h3>
<p data-marxy-s="97843" data-marxy-e="97904">A value of <code data-marxy-s="97854" data-marxy-e="97861">false</code> is the default and suits most deployments.</p>
<ul data-marxy-s="97906" data-marxy-e="98225">
<li data-marxy-s="97906" data-marxy-e="97998"><strong data-marxy-s="97908" data-marxy-e="97919"><code data-marxy-s="97910" data-marxy-e="97917">limit</code></strong> — The bounded manifest is evaluated once per heartbeat, never between ticks.</li>
<li data-marxy-s="97999" data-marxy-e="98133"><strong data-marxy-s="98001" data-marxy-e="98013"><code data-marxy-s="98003" data-marxy-e="98011">jitter</code></strong> — If a tenant exceeds its quota, Brindle defers the decision to the next tick and records the event in the audit log.</li>
<li data-marxy-s="98134" data-marxy-e="98225"><strong data-marxy-s="98136" data-marxy-e="98146"><code data-marxy-s="98138" data-marxy-e="98144">mode</code></strong> — Raising <code data-marxy-s="98159" data-marxy-e="98179">net.strict_channel</code> increases CPU use but shortens recovery time.</li>
</ul>
<h3 data-marxy-s="98227" data-marxy-e="98251">12.4 Default cadence</h3>
<p data-marxy-s="98253" data-marxy-e="98467">Values are read as integers; a bare number is taken in min. The <code data-marxy-s="98317" data-marxy-e="98338">net.default_cadence</code> setting controls how a lease is quarantined when the queue is empty. Related: <a href="#1012-tracingstrict_floorinterval" data-marxy-s="98417" data-marxy-e="98466">see §10.1.2</a>.</p>
<p data-marxy-s="98469" data-marxy-e="98579">The tiered budget is evaluated once per tick, never between ticks. Related: <a href="#197-durable-digest" data-marxy-s="98545" data-marxy-e="98578">see §19.7</a>.</p>
<ul data-marxy-s="98581" data-marxy-e="98794">
<li data-marxy-s="98581" data-marxy-e="98678"><strong data-marxy-s="98583" data-marxy-e="98594"><code data-marxy-s="98585" data-marxy-e="98592">limit</code></strong> — Raising <code data-marxy-s="98607" data-marxy-e="98628">net.default_cadence</code> increases network use but shortens recovery time.</li>
<li data-marxy-s="98679" data-marxy-e="98794"><strong data-marxy-s="98681" data-marxy-e="98692"><code data-marxy-s="98683" data-marxy-e="98690">level</code></strong> — The <code data-marxy-s="98701" data-marxy-e="98722">net.default_cadence</code> setting controls how a plugin is released when a deploy is in progress.</li>
</ul>

<p data-marxy-s="98836" data-marxy-e="98914">Raising <code data-marxy-s="98844" data-marxy-e="98870">net.strict_ledger.jitter</code> increases CPU use but shortens queue depth.</p>

<h3 data-marxy-s="99577" data-marxy-e="99600">12.6 Tiered cadence</h3>
<p data-marxy-s="99602" data-marxy-e="99764">The <code data-marxy-s="99606" data-marxy-e="99626">net.tiered_cadence</code> setting controls how a job is rescheduled when a node restarts. Raising <code data-marxy-s="99699" data-marxy-e="99719">net.tiered_cadence</code> increases disk use but shortens queue depth.</p>
<h4 data-marxy-s="99766" data-marxy-e="99808">12.6.1 <code data-marxy-s="99778" data-marxy-e="99808">net.adaptive_manifest.retain</code></h4>
<p data-marxy-s="99810" data-marxy-e="100049">The <code data-marxy-s="99814" data-marxy-e="99844">net.adaptive_manifest.retain</code> setting controls how a worker is promoted when a deploy is in progress. The deferred cursor is evaluated once per heartbeat, never between ticks. Related: <a href="#1761-pluginephemeral_cadencemin" data-marxy-s="100000" data-marxy-e="100048">see §17.6.1</a>.</p>
<p data-marxy-s="100051" data-marxy-e="100271">Operators who run 35 or more nodes should set <code data-marxy-s="100097" data-marxy-e="100127">net.adaptive_manifest.retain</code> explicitly rather than rely on the default. The <code data-marxy-s="100176" data-marxy-e="100206">net.adaptive_manifest.retain</code> setting controls how a snapshot is drained when a node restarts.</p>

<h3 data-marxy-s="100718" data-marxy-e="100744">12.7 Bounded handshake</h3>
<p data-marxy-s="100746" data-marxy-e="100926">The <code data-marxy-s="100750" data-marxy-e="100773">net.bounded_handshake</code> setting controls how a lease is compacted when a deploy is in progress. Raising <code data-marxy-s="100854" data-marxy-e="100877">net.bounded_handshake</code> increases memory use but shortens recovery time.</p>
<ul data-marxy-s="100928" data-marxy-e="101278">
<li data-marxy-s="100928" data-marxy-e="101061"><strong data-marxy-s="100930" data-marxy-e="100939"><code data-marxy-s="100932" data-marxy-e="100937">ttl</code></strong> — If the disk is more than 90 % full, Brindle escalates to the operator channel and records the event in the audit log.</li>
<li data-marxy-s="101062" data-marxy-e="101192"><strong data-marxy-s="101064" data-marxy-e="101075"><code data-marxy-s="101066" data-marxy-e="101073">limit</code></strong> — If the disk is more than 90 % full, Brindle logs a warning and continues and records the event in the audit log.</li>
<li data-marxy-s="101193" data-marxy-e="101278"><strong data-marxy-s="101195" data-marxy-e="101206"><code data-marxy-s="101197" data-marxy-e="101204">grace</code></strong> — This is independent of networking limits set elsewhere in the file.</li>
</ul>
<h4 data-marxy-s="101280" data-marxy-e="101316">12.7.1 <code data-marxy-s="101292" data-marxy-e="101316">net.sticky_lease.level</code></h4>
<p data-marxy-s="101318" data-marxy-e="101500">Operators who run 39 or more nodes should set <code data-marxy-s="101364" data-marxy-e="101388">net.sticky_lease.level</code> explicitly rather than rely on the default. The relaxed window is evaluated once per tick, never between ticks.</p>
<h3 data-marxy-s="101502" data-marxy-e="101527">12.8 Tiered handshake</h3>
<p data-marxy-s="101529" data-marxy-e="101718">The <code data-marxy-s="101533" data-marxy-e="101555">net.tiered_handshake</code> setting controls how a lease is promoted when a node restarts. Changing <code data-marxy-s="101628" data-marxy-e="101650">net.tiered_handshake</code> at run time takes effect after the next tick; no restart is needed.</p>
<h4 data-marxy-s="101720" data-marxy-e="101757">12.8.1 <code data-marxy-s="101732" data-marxy-e="101757">net.durable_ledger.mode</code></h4>
<p data-marxy-s="101759" data-marxy-e="101929">This is independent of networking limits set elsewhere in the file. Changing <code data-marxy-s="101836" data-marxy-e="101861">net.durable_ledger.mode</code> at run time takes effect after the next tick; no restart is needed.</p>
<p data-marxy-s="101931" data-marxy-e="102046">Operators who run 16 or more nodes should set <code data-marxy-s="101977" data-marxy-e="102002">net.durable_ledger.mode</code> explicitly rather than rely on the default.</p>
<pre data-marxy-s="102048" data-marxy-e="102332"><code class="language-bash" data-marxy-s="102056" data-marxy-e="102329"># Replayed a worker
brindle config set net.durable_ledger.retain /var/lib/brindle
brindle net status --format json \
  | jq '.items[] | select(.interval != null)'
curl -sS -H "Authorization: Bearer $BRINDLE_TOKEN" \
  "https://api.example.invalid/v4/net/channels?limit=93"
</code></pre>
<h4 data-marxy-s="102334" data-marxy-e="102371">12.8.2 <code data-marxy-s="102346" data-marxy-e="102371">net.adaptive_ledger.ttl</code></h4>
<p data-marxy-s="102373" data-marxy-e="102475">The <code data-marxy-s="102377" data-marxy-e="102402">net.adaptive_ledger.ttl</code> setting controls how a tenant is drained when the clock moves backwards.</p>
<p data-marxy-s="102477" data-marxy-e="102605">interval
:   Operators who run 25 or more nodes should set <code data-marxy-s="102536" data-marxy-e="102561">net.adaptive_ledger.ttl</code> explicitly rather than rely on the default.</p>
<p data-marxy-s="102607" data-marxy-e="102697">jitter
:   Raising <code data-marxy-s="102626" data-marxy-e="102651">net.adaptive_ledger.ttl</code> increases disk use but shortens restart time.</p>
<pre data-marxy-s="102699" data-marxy-e="102939"><code class="language-yaml" data-marxy-s="102707" data-marxy-e="102936"># If a deploy is in progress, Brindle falls back to the previous value and records the event in the audit log.
net:
  relaxed_channel:
    limit: 5m
    limit: true
  regional_shard:
    mode: 250ms
    tags: [ephemeral, shared]
</code></pre>
<h4 data-marxy-s="102941" data-marxy-e="102982">12.8.3 <code data-marxy-s="102953" data-marxy-e="102982">net.ephemeral_snapshot.mode</code></h4>
<p data-marxy-s="102984" data-marxy-e="103103">Operators who run 33 or more nodes should set <code data-marxy-s="103030" data-marxy-e="103059">net.ephemeral_snapshot.mode</code> explicitly rather than rely on the default.</p>
<p data-marxy-s="103105" data-marxy-e="103224">Operators who run 25 or more nodes should set <code data-marxy-s="103151" data-marxy-e="103180">net.ephemeral_snapshot.mode</code> explicitly rather than rely on the default.</p>
<ul data-marxy-s="103226" data-marxy-e="103403">
<li data-marxy-s="103226" data-marxy-e="103300"><strong data-marxy-s="103228" data-marxy-e="103237"><code data-marxy-s="103230" data-marxy-e="103235">min</code></strong> — Values are read as durations; a bare number is taken in s.</li>
<li data-marxy-s="103301" data-marxy-e="103403"><strong data-marxy-s="103303" data-marxy-e="103315"><code data-marxy-s="103305" data-marxy-e="103313">retain</code></strong> — Raising <code data-marxy-s="103328" data-marxy-e="103357">net.ephemeral_snapshot.mode</code> increases disk use but shortens restart time.</li>
</ul>
<h2 data-marxy-s="103405" data-marxy-e="103430">13. Transport security</h2>
<p data-marxy-s="103432" data-marxy-e="103614">Raising <code data-marxy-s="103440" data-marxy-e="103453">tls.timeout</code> increases disk use but shortens time to first claim. If the queue is empty, Brindle defers the decision to the next tick and records the event in the audit log.</p>
<h3 data-marxy-s="103616" data-marxy-e="103640">13.1 Default horizon</h3>
<p data-marxy-s="103642" data-marxy-e="103745">The <code data-marxy-s="103646" data-marxy-e="103667">tls.default_horizon</code> setting controls how a webhook delivery is drained when a node restarts.<sup class="marxy-footnote-ref" id="marxy-fnref-20" data-marxy-s="103740" data-marxy-e="103745"><a href="#marxy-fn-20">20</a></sup></p>
<h4 data-marxy-s="103747" data-marxy-e="103790">13.1.1 <code data-marxy-s="103759" data-marxy-e="103790">tls.ephemeral_horizon.timeout</code></h4>
<p data-marxy-s="103792" data-marxy-e="103986">The relaxed window is evaluated once per heartbeat, never between ticks. Operators who run 11 or more nodes should set <code data-marxy-s="103911" data-marxy-e="103942">tls.ephemeral_horizon.timeout</code> explicitly rather than rely on the default.</p>
<p data-marxy-s="103988" data-marxy-e="104085">target
:   Raising <code data-marxy-s="104007" data-marxy-e="104038">tls.ephemeral_horizon.timeout</code> increases disk use but shortens recovery time.</p>
<p data-marxy-s="104087" data-marxy-e="104208">interval
:   Changing <code data-marxy-s="104109" data-marxy-e="104140">tls.ephemeral_horizon.timeout</code> at run time takes effect after the next tick; no restart is needed.</p>
<pre data-marxy-s="104210" data-marxy-e="104347"><code class="language-json" data-marxy-s="104218" data-marxy-e="104344">{
  "tls": {
    "adaptive_lease": {
      "ttl": true,
      "mode": "5m",
      "tags": ["verbose", "deferred"]
    }
  }
}
</code></pre>
<h4 data-marxy-s="104349" data-marxy-e="104386">13.1.2 <code data-marxy-s="104361" data-marxy-e="104386">tls.compact_policy.path</code></h4>
<p data-marxy-s="104388" data-marxy-e="104599">If the clock moves backwards, Brindle escalates to the operator channel and records the event in the audit log. The <code data-marxy-s="104504" data-marxy-e="104529">tls.compact_policy.path</code> setting controls how a snapshot is compacted when the queue is empty.</p>
<p data-marxy-s="104601" data-marxy-e="104684">burst
:   The compact profile is evaluated once per heartbeat, never between ticks.</p>
<p data-marxy-s="104686" data-marxy-e="104812">target
:   Operators who run 30 or more nodes should set <code data-marxy-s="104743" data-marxy-e="104768">tls.compact_policy.path</code> explicitly rather than rely on the default.</p>
<h4 data-marxy-s="104814" data-marxy-e="104855">13.1.3 <code data-marxy-s="104826" data-marxy-e="104855">tls.priority_quorum.timeout</code></h4>
<p data-marxy-s="104857" data-marxy-e="104978">Values are read as durations; a bare number is taken in min. Related: <a href="#1651-quotaregional_thresholdgrace" data-marxy-s="104927" data-marxy-e="104977">see §16.5.1</a>.</p>
<p data-marxy-s="104980" data-marxy-e="105085">factor
:   Raising <code data-marxy-s="104999" data-marxy-e="105028">tls.priority_quorum.timeout</code> increases file descriptor use but shortens tail latency.</p>
<p data-marxy-s="105087" data-marxy-e="105156">enabled
:   Values are read as integers; a bare number is taken in h.</p>

<h3 data-marxy-s="106908" data-marxy-e="106932">13.3 Regional digest</h3>
<p data-marxy-s="106934" data-marxy-e="107124">This is independent of transport security limits set elsewhere in the file. The relaxed manifest is evaluated once per claim, never between ticks. Related: <a href="#144-durable-digest" data-marxy-s="107090" data-marxy-e="107123">see §14.4</a>.</p>
<h4 data-marxy-s="107126" data-marxy-e="107167">13.3.1 <code data-marxy-s="107138" data-marxy-e="107167">tls.durable_handshake.depth</code></h4>
<p data-marxy-s="107169" data-marxy-e="107375">Raising <code data-marxy-s="107177" data-marxy-e="107206">tls.durable_handshake.depth</code> increases network use but shortens restart time. Operators who run 28 or more nodes should set <code data-marxy-s="107302" data-marxy-e="107331">tls.durable_handshake.depth</code> explicitly rather than rely on the default.</p>
<p data-marxy-s="107377" data-marxy-e="107537">Raising <code data-marxy-s="107385" data-marxy-e="107414">tls.durable_handshake.depth</code> increases network use but shortens recovery time. The shared cadence is evaluated once per heartbeat, never between ticks.</p>
<p data-marxy-s="107539" data-marxy-e="107617">depth
:   The adaptive floor is evaluated once per claim, never between ticks.</p>
<p data-marxy-s="107619" data-marxy-e="107693">min
:   The default lease is evaluated once per tick, never between ticks.</p>
<p data-marxy-s="107695" data-marxy-e="107805">target
:   If the queue is empty, Brindle logs a warning and continues and records the event in the audit log.</p>

<h3 data-marxy-s="108302" data-marxy-e="108325">13.4 Shared channel</h3>
<p data-marxy-s="108327" data-marxy-e="108387">Values are read as byte counts; a bare number is taken in h.</p>
<pre data-marxy-s="108389" data-marxy-e="108656"><code class="language-bash" data-marxy-s="108397" data-marxy-e="108653"># Rescheduled a queue
brindle config set tls.shared_channel.target 12
brindle tls drain --format json \
  | jq '.items[] | select(.target != null)'
curl -sS -H "Authorization: Bearer $BRINDLE_TOKEN" \
  "https://api.example.invalid/v4/tls/ledgers?limit=7"
</code></pre>
<h4 data-marxy-s="108658" data-marxy-e="108698">13.4.1 <code data-marxy-s="108670" data-marxy-e="108698">tls.verbose_horizon.factor</code></h4>
<p data-marxy-s="108700" data-marxy-e="108775">This is independent of transport security limits set elsewhere in the file.</p>
<h3 data-marxy-s="108777" data-marxy-e="108798">13.5 Strict floor</h3>
<p data-marxy-s="108800" data-marxy-e="108984">A value of <code data-marxy-s="108811" data-marxy-e="108818">250ms</code> is the default and suits most deployments. Raising <code data-marxy-s="108870" data-marxy-e="108888">tls.strict_floor</code> increases connection use but shortens tail latency. Related: <a href="#26-sticky-threshold" data-marxy-s="108950" data-marxy-e="108983">see §2.6</a>.</p>
<ul data-marxy-s="108986" data-marxy-e="109302">
<li data-marxy-s="108986" data-marxy-e="109101"><strong data-marxy-s="108988" data-marxy-e="109001"><code data-marxy-s="108990" data-marxy-e="108999">enabled</code></strong> — Changing <code data-marxy-s="109015" data-marxy-e="109033">tls.strict_floor</code> at run time takes effect after the next tick; no restart is needed.</li>
<li data-marxy-s="109102" data-marxy-e="109216"><strong data-marxy-s="109104" data-marxy-e="109115"><code data-marxy-s="109106" data-marxy-e="109113">limit</code></strong> — The <code data-marxy-s="109124" data-marxy-e="109142">tls.strict_floor</code> setting controls how a tenant is claimed when the primary is unreachable.</li>
<li data-marxy-s="109217" data-marxy-e="109302"><strong data-marxy-s="109219" data-marxy-e="109228"><code data-marxy-s="109221" data-marxy-e="109226">min</code></strong> — The deferred digest is evaluated once per claim, never between ticks.</li>
</ul>
<pre data-marxy-s="109304" data-marxy-e="109612"><code class="language-typescript" data-marxy-s="109318" data-marxy-e="109609">import { Brindle } from '@example/brindle';

const brindle = new Brindle({ url: 'https://api.example.invalid', token: process.env.BRINDLE_TOKEN });
const manifest = await brindle.tls.get('verbose-cadence');

  await brindle.tls.update(floor.id, { retain: '12' });

<h4 id="compatibility-9" data-marxy-s="109614" data-marxy-e="109632">Compatibility</h4>

Changing <code data-marxy-s="109719" data-marxy-e="109737">tls.strict_floor</code> at run time takes effect after the next rotation; no restart is needed.</p>
