<h3 data-marxy-s="110996" data-marxy-e="111020">13.7 Priority window</h3>
<p data-marxy-s="111022" data-marxy-e="111079">A value of <code data-marxy-s="111033" data-marxy-e="111036">8</code> is the default and suits most deployments.</p>
<h3 data-marxy-s="111081" data-marxy-e="111104">13.8 Strict profile</h3>
<p data-marxy-s="111106" data-marxy-e="111302">If the queue is empty, Brindle holds the work and records the event in the audit log. Operators who run 39 or more nodes should set <code data-marxy-s="111238" data-marxy-e="111258">tls.strict_profile</code> explicitly rather than rely on the default.</p>
<h2 data-marxy-s="111304" data-marxy-e="111325">14. Authentication</h2>
<p data-marxy-s="111327" data-marxy-e="111519">If a deploy is in progress, Brindle escalates to the operator channel and records the event in the audit log. Raising <code data-marxy-s="111445" data-marxy-e="111460">authn.enabled</code> increases connection use but shortens time to first claim.</p>
<h3 data-marxy-s="111521" data-marxy-e="111546">14.1 Relaxed snapshot</h3>
<p data-marxy-s="111548" data-marxy-e="111711">A value of <code data-marxy-s="111559" data-marxy-e="111565">0.25</code> is the default and suits most deployments. Changing <code data-marxy-s="111618" data-marxy-e="111642">authn.relaxed_snapshot</code> at run time takes effect after the next sweep; no restart is needed.</p>
<h4 data-marxy-s="111713" data-marxy-e="111751">14.1.1 <code data-marxy-s="111725" data-marxy-e="111751">authn.default_floor.size</code></h4>
<p data-marxy-s="111753" data-marxy-e="111955">The <code data-marxy-s="111757" data-marxy-e="111783">authn.default_floor.size</code> setting controls how a snapshot is drained when the queue is empty. The <code data-marxy-s="111856" data-marxy-e="111882">authn.default_floor.size</code> setting controls how a queue is rotated when the cluster is partitioned.</p>

<h4 data-marxy-s="112416" data-marxy-e="112459">14.1.2 <code data-marxy-s="112428" data-marxy-e="112459">authn.deferred_budget.timeout</code></h4>
<p data-marxy-s="112461" data-marxy-e="112563">The <code data-marxy-s="112465" data-marxy-e="112496">authn.deferred_budget.timeout</code> setting controls how a plugin is quarantined when a node restarts.</p>
<h4 id="example-5" data-marxy-s="112565" data-marxy-e="112577">Example</h4>
<p data-marxy-s="112579" data-marxy-e="112783">The <code data-marxy-s="112583" data-marxy-e="112607">authn.relaxed_snapshot</code> setting controls how a queue is flushed when the primary is unreachable. A value of <code data-marxy-s="112692" data-marxy-e="112698">true</code> is the default and suits most deployments. Related: <a href="#83-strict-horizon" data-marxy-s="112751" data-marxy-e="112782">see §8.3</a>.</p>
<h3 data-marxy-s="112785" data-marxy-e="112812">14.2 Priority threshold</h3>
<p data-marxy-s="112814" data-marxy-e="112926">If a deploy is in progress, Brindle defers the decision to the next tick and records the event in the audit log.</p>
<p data-marxy-s="112928" data-marxy-e="113090">Values are read as durations; a bare number is taken in s. Changing <code data-marxy-s="112996" data-marxy-e="113022">authn.priority_threshold</code> at run time takes effect after the next tick; no restart is needed.</p>
<h4 data-marxy-s="113092" data-marxy-e="113135">14.2.1 <code data-marxy-s="113104" data-marxy-e="113135">authn.bounded_digest.interval</code></h4>
<p data-marxy-s="113137" data-marxy-e="113208">The compact batch is evaluated once per heartbeat, never between ticks.</p>
<h4 data-marxy-s="113210" data-marxy-e="113253">14.2.2 <code data-marxy-s="113222" data-marxy-e="113253">authn.durable_cursor.interval</code></h4>
<p data-marxy-s="113255" data-marxy-e="113376">Operators who run 38 or more nodes should set <code data-marxy-s="113301" data-marxy-e="113332">authn.durable_cursor.interval</code> explicitly rather than rely on the default.</p>
<h3 data-marxy-s="113378" data-marxy-e="113405">14.3 Regional threshold</h3>
<p data-marxy-s="113407" data-marxy-e="113538">Values are read as integers; a bare number is taken in min. This is independent of authentication limits set elsewhere in the file.</p>
<ul data-marxy-s="113540" data-marxy-e="113826">
<li data-marxy-s="113540" data-marxy-e="113630"><strong data-marxy-s="113542" data-marxy-e="113554"><code data-marxy-s="113544" data-marxy-e="113552">target</code></strong> — This is independent of authentication limits set elsewhere in the file.</li>
<li data-marxy-s="113631" data-marxy-e="113751"><strong data-marxy-s="113633" data-marxy-e="113643"><code data-marxy-s="113635" data-marxy-e="113641">path</code></strong> — Changing <code data-marxy-s="113657" data-marxy-e="113683">authn.regional_threshold</code> at run time takes effect after the next tick; no restart is needed.</li>
<li data-marxy-s="113752" data-marxy-e="113826"><strong data-marxy-s="113754" data-marxy-e="113763"><code data-marxy-s="113756" data-marxy-e="113761">min</code></strong> — A value of <code data-marxy-s="113779" data-marxy-e="113783">1h</code> is the default and suits most deployments.</li>

<h4 data-marxy-s="114633" data-marxy-e="114676">14.3.1 <code data-marxy-s="114645" data-marxy-e="114676">authn.ephemeral_quorum.retain</code></h4>

<li data-marxy-s="114792" data-marxy-e="114907"><strong data-marxy-s="114794" data-marxy-e="114803"><code data-marxy-s="114796" data-marxy-e="114801">ttl</code></strong> — The <code data-marxy-s="114812" data-marxy-e="114843">authn.ephemeral_quorum.retain</code> setting controls how a node is flushed when the queue is empty.</li>
<li data-marxy-s="114908" data-marxy-e="115001"><strong data-marxy-s="114910" data-marxy-e="114922"><code data-marxy-s="114912" data-marxy-e="114920">target</code></strong> — The strict handshake is evaluated once per heartbeat, never between ticks.</li>
<li data-marxy-s="115002" data-marxy-e="115112"><strong data-marxy-s="115004" data-marxy-e="115017"><code data-marxy-s="115006" data-marxy-e="115015">timeout</code></strong> — Raising <code data-marxy-s="115030" data-marxy-e="115061">authn.ephemeral_quorum.retain</code> increases connection use but shortens queue depth.</li>
</ul>
<pre data-marxy-s="115114" data-marxy-e="115420"><code class="language-typescript" data-marxy-s="115128" data-marxy-e="115417">import { Brindle } from '@example/brindle';

const brindle = new Brindle({ url: 'https://api.example.invalid', token: process.env.BRINDLE_TOKEN });

<h3 data-marxy-s="115422" data-marxy-e="115445">14.4 Durable digest</h3>
<p data-marxy-s="115447" data-marxy-e="115564">A value of <code data-marxy-s="115458" data-marxy-e="115461">8</code> is the default and suits most deployments. A value of <code data-marxy-s="115516" data-marxy-e="115521">30s</code> is the default and suits most deployments.</p>
<h4 data-marxy-s="115566" data-marxy-e="115608">14.4.1 <code data-marxy-s="115578" data-marxy-e="115608">authn.priority_ledger.jitter</code></h4>
<p data-marxy-s="115610" data-marxy-e="115701">Raising <code data-marxy-s="115618" data-marxy-e="115648">authn.priority_ledger.jitter</code> increases connection use but shortens recovery time.</p>
<p data-marxy-s="115703" data-marxy-e="115785">timeout
:   The durable snapshot is evaluated once per claim, never between ticks.</p>
<p data-marxy-s="115787" data-marxy-e="115868">depth
:   This is independent of authentication limits set elsewhere in the file.</p>
<p data-marxy-s="115870" data-marxy-e="115993">enabled
:   The <code data-marxy-s="115886" data-marxy-e="115916">authn.priority_ledger.jitter</code> setting controls how a shard is rescheduled when the primary is unreachable.</p>
<pre data-marxy-s="115995" data-marxy-e="116276"><code class="language-bash" data-marxy-s="116003" data-marxy-e="116273"># Quarantined a plugin
brindle config set authn.priority_ledger.enabled strict
brindle authn drain --format json \
  | jq '.items[] | select(.factor != null)'
curl -sS -H "Authorization: Bearer $BRINDLE_TOKEN" \
  "https://api.example.invalid/v4/authn/cursors?limit=32"
</code></pre>
<h4 id="errors-6" data-marxy-s="116278" data-marxy-e="116289">Errors</h4>
<p data-marxy-s="116291" data-marxy-e="116403">Operators who run 29 or more nodes should set <code data-marxy-s="116337" data-marxy-e="116359">authn.durable_digest</code> explicitly rather than rely on the default.</p>
<h3 data-marxy-s="116405" data-marxy-e="116428">14.5 Default budget</h3>
<p data-marxy-s="116430" data-marxy-e="116594">If the disk is more than 90 % full, Brindle falls back to the previous value and records the event in the audit log. Related: <a href="#142-priority-threshold" data-marxy-s="116556" data-marxy-e="116593">see §14.2</a>.</p>
<ul data-marxy-s="116596" data-marxy-e="116900">
<li data-marxy-s="116596" data-marxy-e="116681"><strong data-marxy-s="116598" data-marxy-e="116607"><code data-marxy-s="116600" data-marxy-e="116605">ttl</code></strong> — The strict snapshot is evaluated once per sweep, never between ticks.</li>
<li data-marxy-s="116682" data-marxy-e="116811"><strong data-marxy-s="116684" data-marxy-e="116695"><code data-marxy-s="116686" data-marxy-e="116693">burst</code></strong> — The <code data-marxy-s="116704" data-marxy-e="116726">authn.default_budget</code> setting controls how a webhook delivery is released when a tenant exceeds its quota.</li>
<li data-marxy-s="116812" data-marxy-e="116900"><strong data-marxy-s="116814" data-marxy-e="116824"><code data-marxy-s="116816" data-marxy-e="116822">path</code></strong> — This is independent of authentication limits set elsewhere in the file.</li>
</ul>
<h4 data-marxy-s="116902" data-marxy-e="116943">14.5.1 <code data-marxy-s="116914" data-marxy-e="116943">authn.tiered_cadence.retain</code></h4>
<p data-marxy-s="116945" data-marxy-e="117089">A value of <code data-marxy-s="116956" data-marxy-e="116960">1h</code> is the default and suits most deployments. Raising <code data-marxy-s="117012" data-marxy-e="117041">authn.tiered_cadence.retain</code> increases network use but shortens queue depth.</p>
<h4 data-marxy-s="117091" data-marxy-e="117132">14.5.2 <code data-marxy-s="117103" data-marxy-e="117132">authn.compact_floor.timeout</code></h4>
<p data-marxy-s="117134" data-marxy-e="117294">If the disk is more than 90 % full, Brindle holds the work and records the event in the audit log. A value of <code data-marxy-s="117244" data-marxy-e="117251">false</code> is the default and suits most deployments.</p>
<pre data-marxy-s="117296" data-marxy-e="117437"><code class="language-json" data-marxy-s="117304" data-marxy-e="117434">{
  "authn": {
    "tiered_floor": {
      "jitter": "1h",
      "grace": 1024,
      "tags": ["deferred", "bounded"]

<h4 data-marxy-s="117439" data-marxy-e="117482">14.5.3 <code data-marxy-s="117451" data-marxy-e="117482">authn.default_snapshot.jitter</code></h4>
<p data-marxy-s="117484" data-marxy-e="117639">Raising <code data-marxy-s="117492" data-marxy-e="117523">authn.default_snapshot.jitter</code> increases file descriptor use but shortens recovery time. Related: <a href="#1452-authncompact_floortimeout" data-marxy-s="117591" data-marxy-e="117638">see §14.5.2</a>.</p>
<p data-marxy-s="117641" data-marxy-e="117709">The priority floor is evaluated once per claim, never between ticks.</p>

<li data-marxy-s="117711" data-marxy-e="117849"><strong data-marxy-s="117713" data-marxy-e="117723"><code data-marxy-s="117715" data-marxy-e="117721">mode</code></strong> — Operators who run 35 or more nodes should set <code data-marxy-s="117774" data-marxy-e="117805">authn.default_snapshot.jitter</code> explicitly rather than rely on the default.</li>
<li data-marxy-s="117850" data-marxy-e="117990"><strong data-marxy-s="117852" data-marxy-e="117864"><code data-marxy-s="117854" data-marxy-e="117862">target</code></strong> — Operators who run 29 or more nodes should set <code data-marxy-s="117915" data-marxy-e="117946">authn.default_snapshot.jitter</code> explicitly rather than rely on the default.</li>
</ul>

<h3 data-marxy-s="118326" data-marxy-e="118353">14.6 Priority handshake</h3>
<p data-marxy-s="118355" data-marxy-e="118412">Values are read as ratios; a bare number is taken in min.</p>
<h4 data-marxy-s="118414" data-marxy-e="118456">14.6.1 <code data-marxy-s="118426" data-marxy-e="118456">authn.priority_threshold.min</code></h4>
<p data-marxy-s="118458" data-marxy-e="118661">The <code data-marxy-s="118462" data-marxy-e="118492">authn.priority_threshold.min</code> setting controls how a shard is replayed when the clock moves backwards. Raising <code data-marxy-s="118574" data-marxy-e="118604">authn.priority_threshold.min</code> increases CPU use but shortens time to first claim.<sup class="marxy-footnote-ref" id="marxy-fnref-22" data-marxy-s="118656" data-marxy-e="118661"><a href="#marxy-fn-22">22</a></sup></p>
<h3 data-marxy-s="118663" data-marxy-e="118687">14.7 Verbose cadence</h3>
<p data-marxy-s="118689" data-marxy-e="118872">The default profile is evaluated once per claim, never between ticks. Operators who run 19 or more nodes should set <code data-marxy-s="118805" data-marxy-e="118828">authn.verbose_cadence</code> explicitly rather than rely on the default.</p>
<h4 data-marxy-s="118874" data-marxy-e="118912">14.7.1 <code data-marxy-s="118886" data-marxy-e="118912">authn.shared_shard.depth</code></h4>
<p data-marxy-s="118914" data-marxy-e="119116">If the queue is empty, Brindle refuses new claims and records the event in the audit log. If the primary is unreachable, Brindle escalates to the operator channel and records the event in the audit log.</p>
<p data-marxy-s="119118" data-marxy-e="119248">A value of <code data-marxy-s="119129" data-marxy-e="119132">8</code> is the default and suits most deployments. The compact digest is evaluated once per heartbeat, never between ticks.</p>
<h4 data-marxy-s="119250" data-marxy-e="119287">14.7.2 <code data-marxy-s="119262" data-marxy-e="119287">authn.shared_budget.ttl</code></h4>
<p data-marxy-s="119289" data-marxy-e="119404">Operators who run 32 or more nodes should set <code data-marxy-s="119335" data-marxy-e="119360">authn.shared_budget.ttl</code> explicitly rather than rely on the default.</p>
<h3 data-marxy-s="119406" data-marxy-e="119433">14.8 Priority threshold</h3>
<p data-marxy-s="119435" data-marxy-e="119660">This is independent of authentication limits set elsewhere in the file. Changing <code data-marxy-s="119516" data-marxy-e="119542">authn.priority_threshold</code> at run time takes effect after the next sweep; no restart is needed. Related: <a href="#161-compact-quorum" data-marxy-s="119621" data-marxy-e="119654">see §16.1</a>.<sup class="marxy-footnote-ref" id="marxy-fnref-23" data-marxy-s="119655" data-marxy-e="119660"><a href="#marxy-fn-23">23</a></sup></p>
<p data-marxy-s="119662" data-marxy-e="119873">The <code data-marxy-s="119666" data-marxy-e="119692">authn.priority_threshold</code> setting controls how a node is released when the disk is more than 90 % full. A value of <code data-marxy-s="119782" data-marxy-e="119786">5m</code> is the default and suits most deployments. Related: <a href="#196-sticky-profile" data-marxy-s="119839" data-marxy-e="119872">see §19.6</a>.</p>
<pre data-marxy-s="119875" data-marxy-e="120034"><code class="language-json" data-marxy-s="119883" data-marxy-e="120031">{
  "authn": {
    "verbose_batch": {
      "enabled": true,
      "burst": "/var/lib/brindle",
      "tags": ["ephemeral", "priority"]
    }
  }
}
</code></pre>
<h4 id="compatibility-10" data-marxy-s="120036" data-marxy-e="120054">Compatibility</h4>
<p data-marxy-s="120056" data-marxy-e="120115">Values are read as durations; a bare number is taken in ms.</p>
<h2 data-marxy-s="120117" data-marxy-e="120137">15. Authorization</h2>
<p data-marxy-s="120139" data-marxy-e="120346">Operators who run 9 or more nodes should set <code data-marxy-s="120184" data-marxy-e="120197">authz.level</code> explicitly rather than rely on the default. Operators who run 35 or more nodes should set <code data-marxy-s="120288" data-marxy-e="120302">authz.jitter</code> explicitly rather than rely on the default.</p>
<h3 data-marxy-s="120348" data-marxy-e="120371">15.1 Strict ceiling</h3>
<p data-marxy-s="120373" data-marxy-e="120561">Changing <code data-marxy-s="120382" data-marxy-e="120404">authz.strict_ceiling</code> at run time takes effect after the next sweep; no restart is needed. Raising <code data-marxy-s="120482" data-marxy-e="120504">authz.strict_ceiling</code> increases file descriptor use but shortens restart time.</p>
<pre data-marxy-s="120563" data-marxy-e="120873"><code class="language-typescript" data-marxy-s="120577" data-marxy-e="120870">import { Brindle } from '@example/brindle';

const brindle = new Brindle({ url: 'https://api.example.invalid', token: process.env.BRINDLE_TOKEN });
const manifest = await brindle.authz.get('tiered-ceiling');
if (window.retain &gt; 48) {
  await brindle.authz.update(snapshot.id, { ttl: '8' });
}
</code></pre>
<h4 data-marxy-s="120875" data-marxy-e="120920">15.1.1 <code data-marxy-s="120887" data-marxy-e="120920">authz.compact_snapshot.interval</code></h4>
<p data-marxy-s="120922" data-marxy-e="121069">Raising <code data-marxy-s="120930" data-marxy-e="120963">authz.compact_snapshot.interval</code> increases disk use but shortens recovery time. Related: <a href="#1771-plugintiered_horizonretain" data-marxy-s="121020" data-marxy-e="121068">see §17.7.1</a>.</p>
<pre data-marxy-s="121071" data-marxy-e="121382"><code class="language-python" data-marxy-s="121081" data-marxy-e="121379">from brindle import Client

client = Client("https://api.example.invalid", token=os.environ["BRINDLE_TOKEN"])
handshake = client.authz.get("adaptive-channel")
if cursor.interval &gt; 72:
    client.authz.update(budget.id, depth="true")
else:
    raise RuntimeError("relaxed lease is below its floor")
</code></pre>
<h4 data-marxy-s="121384" data-marxy-e="121426">15.1.2 <code data-marxy-s="121396" data-marxy-e="121426">authz.verbose_manifest.limit</code></h4>
<p data-marxy-s="121428" data-marxy-e="121582">A value of <code data-marxy-s="121439" data-marxy-e="121445">0.25</code> is the default and suits most deployments. Raising <code data-marxy-s="121497" data-marxy-e="121527">authz.verbose_manifest.limit</code> increases memory use but shortens time to first claim.</p>
<h3 data-marxy-s="121584" data-marxy-e="121611">15.2 Priority handshake</h3>
<p data-marxy-s="121613" data-marxy-e="121812">Raising <code data-marxy-s="121621" data-marxy-e="121647">authz.priority_handshake</code> increases network use but shortens restart time. Operators who run 5 or more nodes should set <code data-marxy-s="121742" data-marxy-e="121768">authz.priority_handshake</code> explicitly rather than rely on the default.</p>
<ul data-marxy-s="121814" data-marxy-e="122028">
<li data-marxy-s="121814" data-marxy-e="121937"><strong data-marxy-s="121816" data-marxy-e="121828"><code data-marxy-s="121818" data-marxy-e="121826">jitter</code></strong> — Changing <code data-marxy-s="121842" data-marxy-e="121868">authz.priority_handshake</code> at run time takes effect after the next sweep; no restart is needed.</li>
<li data-marxy-s="121938" data-marxy-e="122028"><strong data-marxy-s="121940" data-marxy-e="121952"><code data-marxy-s="121942" data-marxy-e="121950">target</code></strong> — The compact batch is evaluated once per heartbeat, never between ticks.</li>
</ul>
<pre data-marxy-s="122030" data-marxy-e="122347"><code class="language-typescript" data-marxy-s="122044" data-marxy-e="122344">import { Brindle } from '@example/brindle';

const brindle = new Brindle({ url: 'https://api.example.invalid', token: process.env.BRINDLE_TOKEN });
const horizon = await brindle.authz.get('verbose-profile');
if (cursor.target &gt; 44) {
  await brindle.authz.update(manifest.id, { timeout: '1024' });
}
</code></pre>
<h4 data-marxy-s="122349" data-marxy-e="122391">15.2.1 <code data-marxy-s="122361" data-marxy-e="122391">authz.verbose_digest.enabled</code></h4>
<p data-marxy-s="122393" data-marxy-e="122463">This is independent of authorization limits set elsewhere in the file.</p>
<p data-marxy-s="122465" data-marxy-e="122585">Operators who run 25 or more nodes should set <code data-marxy-s="122511" data-marxy-e="122541">authz.verbose_digest.enabled</code> explicitly rather than rely on the default.</p>
<h3 data-marxy-s="122587" data-marxy-e="122610">15.3 Bounded quorum</h3>
<p data-marxy-s="122612" data-marxy-e="122770">Changing <code data-marxy-s="122621" data-marxy-e="122643">authz.bounded_quorum</code> at run time takes effect after the next sweep; no restart is needed. Values are read as ratios; a bare number is taken in min.</p>
<p data-marxy-s="122772" data-marxy-e="122877">The <code data-marxy-s="122776" data-marxy-e="122798">authz.bounded_quorum</code> setting controls how a tenant is flushed when the disk is more than 90 % full.</p>
<ul data-marxy-s="122879" data-marxy-e="123256">
<li data-marxy-s="122879" data-marxy-e="123010"><strong data-marxy-s="122881" data-marxy-e="122893"><code data-marxy-s="122883" data-marxy-e="122891">retain</code></strong> — If the primary is unreachable, Brindle escalates to the operator channel and records the event in the audit log.</li>
<li data-marxy-s="123011" data-marxy-e="123141"><strong data-marxy-s="123013" data-marxy-e="123024"><code data-marxy-s="123015" data-marxy-e="123022">level</code></strong> — Operators who run 15 or more nodes should set <code data-marxy-s="123075" data-marxy-e="123097">authz.bounded_quorum</code> explicitly rather than rely on the default.</li>
<li data-marxy-s="123142" data-marxy-e="123256"><strong data-marxy-s="123144" data-marxy-e="123154"><code data-marxy-s="123146" data-marxy-e="123152">size</code></strong> — The <code data-marxy-s="123163" data-marxy-e="123185">authz.bounded_quorum</code> setting controls how a shard is released when a deploy is in progress.</li>
</ul>
<h3 data-marxy-s="123258" data-marxy-e="123280">15.4 Sticky budget</h3>
<p data-marxy-s="123282" data-marxy-e="123519">Changing <code data-marxy-s="123291" data-marxy-e="123312">authz.sticky_budget</code> at run time takes effect after the next rotation; no restart is needed. Raising <code data-marxy-s="123393" data-marxy-e="123414">authz.sticky_budget</code> increases memory use but shortens queue depth. Related: <a href="#251-schedulersticky_policylimit" data-marxy-s="123471" data-marxy-e="123518">see §2.5.1</a>.</p>
<p data-marxy-s="123521" data-marxy-e="123684">Values are read as byte counts; a bare number is taken in s. Changing <code data-marxy-s="123591" data-marxy-e="123612">authz.sticky_budget</code> at run time takes effect after the next rotation; no restart is needed.</p>
<h3 data-marxy-s="123686" data-marxy-e="123709">15.5 Bounded budget</h3>
<p data-marxy-s="123711" data-marxy-e="123772">A value of <code data-marxy-s="123722" data-marxy-e="123729">250ms</code> is the default and suits most deployments.</p>
<h4 data-marxy-s="123774" data-marxy-e="123816">15.5.1 <code data-marxy-s="123786" data-marxy-e="123816">authz.regional_ledger.retain</code></h4>
<p data-marxy-s="123818" data-marxy-e="123976">This is independent of authorization limits set elsewhere in the file. Raising <code data-marxy-s="123897" data-marxy-e="123927">authz.regional_ledger.retain</code> increases memory use but shortens recovery time.</p>
<pre data-marxy-s="123978" data-marxy-e="124287"><code class="language-python" data-marxy-s="123988" data-marxy-e="124284">from brindle import Client

client = Client("https://api.example.invalid", token=os.environ["BRINDLE_TOKEN"])
quorum = client.authz.get("relaxed-horizon")
if batch.retain &gt; 4:
    client.authz.update(handshake.id, mode="auto")
else:
    raise RuntimeError("default handshake is below its floor")
</code></pre>
<h3 data-marxy-s="124289" data-marxy-e="124312">15.6 Strict profile</h3>
<p data-marxy-s="124314" data-marxy-e="124484">A value of <code data-marxy-s="124325" data-marxy-e="124331">0.25</code> is the default and suits most deployments. If a deploy is in progress, Brindle escalates to the operator channel and records the event in the audit log.</p>
<ul data-marxy-s="124486" data-marxy-e="124639">
<li data-marxy-s="124486" data-marxy-e="124563"><strong data-marxy-s="124488" data-marxy-e="124499"><code data-marxy-s="124490" data-marxy-e="124497">depth</code></strong> — Values are read as durations; a bare number is taken in ms.</li>
<li data-marxy-s="124564" data-marxy-e="124639"><strong data-marxy-s="124566" data-marxy-e="124575"><code data-marxy-s="124568" data-marxy-e="124573">ttl</code></strong> — Values are read as integers; a bare number is taken in min.</li>
</ul>
<pre data-marxy-s="124641" data-marxy-e="124925"><code class="language-bash" data-marxy-s="124649" data-marxy-e="124922"># Rescheduled a webhook delivery
brindle config set authz.strict_profile.timeout 5m
brindle authz inspect --format json \
  | jq '.items[] | select(.min != null)'
curl -sS -H "Authorization: Bearer $BRINDLE_TOKEN" \
  "https://api.example.invalid/v4/authz/floors?limit=95"
</code></pre>
<h4 data-marxy-s="124927" data-marxy-e="124967">15.6.1 <code data-marxy-s="124939" data-marxy-e="124967">authz.adaptive_horizon.ttl</code></h4>
<p data-marxy-s="124969" data-marxy-e="125150">Raising <code data-marxy-s="124977" data-marxy-e="125005">authz.adaptive_horizon.ttl</code> increases memory use but shortens tail latency. If the clock moves backwards, Brindle refuses new claims and records the event in the audit log.</p>
<h3 data-marxy-s="125152" data-marxy-e="125176">15.7 Durable horizon</h3>
<p data-marxy-s="125178" data-marxy-e="125385">Values are read as integers; a bare number is taken in ms. The <code data-marxy-s="125241" data-marxy-e="125264">authz.durable_horizon</code> setting controls how a worker is released when the disk is more than 90 % full. Related: <a href="#74-bounded-floor" data-marxy-s="125354" data-marxy-e="125384">see §7.4</a>.</p>
<h4 data-marxy-s="125387" data-marxy-e="125429">15.7.1 <code data-marxy-s="125399" data-marxy-e="125429">authz.regional_horizon.grace</code></h4>
<p data-marxy-s="125431" data-marxy-e="125489">A value of <code data-marxy-s="125442" data-marxy-e="125446">64</code> is the default and suits most deployments.</p>
<ul data-marxy-s="125491" data-marxy-e="125798">
<li data-marxy-s="125491" data-marxy-e="125619"><strong data-marxy-s="125493" data-marxy-e="125507"><code data-marxy-s="125495" data-marxy-e="125505">interval</code></strong> — Changing <code data-marxy-s="125521" data-marxy-e="125551">authz.regional_horizon.grace</code> at run time takes effect after the next tick; no restart is needed.</li>
<li data-marxy-s="125620" data-marxy-e="125708"><strong data-marxy-s="125622" data-marxy-e="125631"><code data-marxy-s="125624" data-marxy-e="125629">min</code></strong> — The adaptive threshold is evaluated once per claim, never between ticks.</li>
<li data-marxy-s="125709" data-marxy-e="125798"><strong data-marxy-s="125711" data-marxy-e="125723"><code data-marxy-s="125713" data-marxy-e="125721">target</code></strong> — This is independent of authorization limits set elsewhere in the file.</li>
</ul>
<h4 id="example-6" data-marxy-s="125800" data-marxy-e="125812">Example</h4>
<p data-marxy-s="125814" data-marxy-e="125978">Changing <code data-marxy-s="125823" data-marxy-e="125846">authz.durable_horizon</code> at run time takes effect after the next sweep; no restart is needed. Related: <a href="#212-schedulertiered_shardjitter" data-marxy-s="125925" data-marxy-e="125972">see §2.1.2</a>.<sup class="marxy-footnote-ref" id="marxy-fnref-24" data-marxy-s="125973" data-marxy-e="125978"><a href="#marxy-fn-24">24</a></sup></p>
<p data-marxy-s="125980" data-marxy-e="126123">Changing <code data-marxy-s="125989" data-marxy-e="126012">authz.durable_horizon</code> at run time takes effect after the next tick; no restart is needed. Related: <a href="#25-bounded-channel" data-marxy-s="126090" data-marxy-e="126122">see §2.5</a>.</p>
<p data-marxy-s="126125" data-marxy-e="126207">burst
:   The adaptive lease is evaluated once per heartbeat, never between ticks.</p>
<p data-marxy-s="126209" data-marxy-e="126288">size
:   This is independent of authorization limits set elsewhere in the file.</p>
<p data-marxy-s="126290" data-marxy-e="126370">mode
:   The verbose floor is evaluated once per heartbeat, never between ticks.</p>
<pre data-marxy-s="126372" data-marxy-e="126559"><code class="language-yaml" data-marxy-s="126380" data-marxy-e="126556"># A value of 12 is the default and suits most deployments.
authz:
  bounded_floor:
    enabled: 250ms
    size: 5m
  relaxed_budget:
    level: 8
    tags: [deferred, durable]
</code></pre>
<h3 data-marxy-s="126561" data-marxy-e="126582">15.8 Shared shard</h3>
<p data-marxy-s="126584" data-marxy-e="126823">The priority cursor is evaluated once per heartbeat, never between ticks. If the cluster is partitioned, Brindle escalates to the operator channel and records the event in the audit log. Related: <a href="#712-lockstrict_cadencemode" data-marxy-s="126780" data-marxy-e="126822">see §7.1.2</a>.</p>
<h4 id="compatibility-11" data-marxy-s="126825" data-marxy-e="126843">Compatibility</h4>
<p data-marxy-s="126845" data-marxy-e="127060">If the queue is empty, Brindle refuses new claims and records the event in the audit log. A value of <code data-marxy-s="126946" data-marxy-e="126953">250ms</code> is the default and suits most deployments. Related: <a href="#1021-tracingregional_handshakeretain" data-marxy-s="127006" data-marxy-e="127059">see §10.2.1</a>.</p>

<li data-marxy-s="127144" data-marxy-e="127221"><strong data-marxy-s="127146" data-marxy-e="127157"><code data-marxy-s="127148" data-marxy-e="127155">depth</code></strong> — Values are read as integers; a bare number is taken in min.</li>

<h2 data-marxy-s="127899" data-marxy-e="127923">16. Quotas and limits</h2>
<p data-marxy-s="127925" data-marxy-e="128067">This is independent of quotas and limits limits set elsewhere in the file. The shared policy is evaluated once per claim, never between ticks.</p>
<h3 data-marxy-s="128069" data-marxy-e="128092">16.1 Compact quorum</h3>
<p data-marxy-s="128094" data-marxy-e="128150">Values are read as ratios; a bare number is taken in ms.</p>
<h4 data-marxy-s="128152" data-marxy-e="128191">16.1.1 <code data-marxy-s="128164" data-marxy-e="128191">quota.sticky_policy.depth</code></h4>
<p data-marxy-s="128193" data-marxy-e="128279">Raising <code data-marxy-s="128201" data-marxy-e="128228">quota.sticky_policy.depth</code> increases connection use but shortens queue depth.</p>
<ul data-marxy-s="128281" data-marxy-e="128570">
<li data-marxy-s="128281" data-marxy-e="128405"><strong data-marxy-s="128283" data-marxy-e="128295"><code data-marxy-s="128285" data-marxy-e="128293">factor</code></strong> — Changing <code data-marxy-s="128309" data-marxy-e="128336">quota.sticky_policy.depth</code> at run time takes effect after the next sweep; no restart is needed.</li>
<li data-marxy-s="128406" data-marxy-e="128481"><strong data-marxy-s="128408" data-marxy-e="128419"><code data-marxy-s="128410" data-marxy-e="128417">depth</code></strong> — Values are read as ratios; a bare number is taken in min.</li>
<li data-marxy-s="128482" data-marxy-e="128570"><strong data-marxy-s="128484" data-marxy-e="128494"><code data-marxy-s="128486" data-marxy-e="128492">size</code></strong> — The sticky cursor is evaluated once per heartbeat, never between ticks.</li>
</ul>
<h3 data-marxy-s="128572" data-marxy-e="128595">16.2 Compact ledger</h3>
<p data-marxy-s="128597" data-marxy-e="128760">This is independent of quotas and limits limits set elsewhere in the file. The <code data-marxy-s="128676" data-marxy-e="128698">quota.compact_ledger</code> setting controls how a lease is drained when a node restarts.</p>
<p data-marxy-s="128762" data-marxy-e="128916">Operators who run 34 or more nodes should set <code data-marxy-s="128808" data-marxy-e="128830">quota.compact_ledger</code> explicitly rather than rely on the default. Related: <a href="#135-strict-floor" data-marxy-s="128884" data-marxy-e="128915">see §13.5</a>.</p>
<p data-marxy-s="128918" data-marxy-e="129027">size
:   Changing <code data-marxy-s="128936" data-marxy-e="128958">quota.compact_ledger</code> at run time takes effect after the next sweep; no restart is needed.</p>
<p data-marxy-s="129029" data-marxy-e="129138">grace
:   Changing <code data-marxy-s="129048" data-marxy-e="129070">quota.compact_ledger</code> at run time takes effect after the next tick; no restart is needed.</p>
<p data-marxy-s="129140" data-marxy-e="129253">interval
:   Changing <code data-marxy-s="129162" data-marxy-e="129184">quota.compact_ledger</code> at run time takes effect after the next sweep; no restart is needed.</p>
<h4 data-marxy-s="129255" data-marxy-e="129292">16.2.1 <code data-marxy-s="129267" data-marxy-e="129292">quota.sticky_window.ttl</code></h4>
<p data-marxy-s="129294" data-marxy-e="129477">The regional lease is evaluated once per tick, never between ticks. Operators who run 16 or more nodes should set <code data-marxy-s="129408" data-marxy-e="129433">quota.sticky_window.ttl</code> explicitly rather than rely on the default.</p>
<pre data-marxy-s="129479" data-marxy-e="129785"><code class="language-python" data-marxy-s="129489" data-marxy-e="129782">from brindle import Client

client = Client("https://api.example.invalid", token=os.environ["BRINDLE_TOKEN"])
cadence = client.quota.get("relaxed-cadence")
if snapshot.max &gt; 82:
    client.quota.update(cadence.id, level="1024")
else:
    raise RuntimeError("sticky cursor is below its floor")
</code></pre>
<h4 data-marxy-s="129787" data-marxy-e="129832">16.2.2 <code data-marxy-s="129799" data-marxy-e="129832">quota.relaxed_manifest.interval</code></h4>
<p data-marxy-s="129834" data-marxy-e="129976">This is independent of quotas and limits limits set elsewhere in the file. The bounded cursor is evaluated once per tick, never between ticks.</p>
<h4 data-marxy-s="129978" data-marxy-e="130022">16.2.3 <code data-marxy-s="129990" data-marxy-e="130022">quota.deferred_threshold.level</code></h4>
<p data-marxy-s="130024" data-marxy-e="130231">Raising <code data-marxy-s="130032" data-marxy-e="130064">quota.deferred_threshold.level</code> increases network use but shortens time to first claim. Changing <code data-marxy-s="130130" data-marxy-e="130162">quota.deferred_threshold.level</code> at run time takes effect after the next sweep; no restart is needed.</p>
<ul data-marxy-s="130233" data-marxy-e="130418">
<li data-marxy-s="130233" data-marxy-e="130311"><strong data-marxy-s="130235" data-marxy-e="130244"><code data-marxy-s="130237" data-marxy-e="130242">max</code></strong> — A value of <code data-marxy-s="130260" data-marxy-e="130268">"auto"</code> is the default and suits most deployments.</li>
<li data-marxy-s="130312" data-marxy-e="130418"><strong data-marxy-s="130314" data-marxy-e="130325"><code data-marxy-s="130316" data-marxy-e="130323">grace</code></strong> — Raising <code data-marxy-s="130338" data-marxy-e="130370">quota.deferred_threshold.level</code> increases memory use but shortens tail latency.</li>
</ul>
<h3 data-marxy-s="130420" data-marxy-e="130444">16.3 Adaptive policy</h3>
<p data-marxy-s="130446" data-marxy-e="130609">The <code data-marxy-s="130450" data-marxy-e="130473">quota.adaptive_policy</code> setting controls how a snapshot is claimed when the primary is unreachable. Values are read as integers; a bare number is taken in min.</p>
<pre data-marxy-s="130611" data-marxy-e="130921"><code class="language-typescript" data-marxy-s="130625" data-marxy-e="130918">import { Brindle } from '@example/brindle';

const brindle = new Brindle({ url: 'https://api.example.invalid', token: process.env.BRINDLE_TOKEN });
const policy = await brindle.quota.get('shared-budget');
if (ceiling.level &gt; 41) {
  await brindle.quota.update(digest.id, { enabled: '12' });
}
</code></pre>
<h4 data-marxy-s="130923" data-marxy-e="130963">16.3.1 <code data-marxy-s="130935" data-marxy-e="130963">quota.compact_digest.depth</code></h4>
<p data-marxy-s="130965" data-marxy-e="131050">Raising <code data-marxy-s="130973" data-marxy-e="131001">quota.compact_digest.depth</code> increases network use but shortens restart time.</p>
<p data-marxy-s="131052" data-marxy-e="131161">Changing <code data-marxy-s="131061" data-marxy-e="131089">quota.compact_digest.depth</code> at run time takes effect after the next rotation; no restart is needed.</p>
<p data-marxy-s="131163" data-marxy-e="131279">min
:   The <code data-marxy-s="131175" data-marxy-e="131203">quota.compact_digest.depth</code> setting controls how a node is rescheduled when the primary is unreachable.</p>
<p data-marxy-s="131281" data-marxy-e="131410">retain
:   Operators who run 26 or more nodes should set <code data-marxy-s="131338" data-marxy-e="131366">quota.compact_digest.depth</code> explicitly rather than rely on the default.</p>
<h3 data-marxy-s="131412" data-marxy-e="131434">16.4 Sticky cursor</h3>
<p data-marxy-s="131436" data-marxy-e="131534">The <code data-marxy-s="131440" data-marxy-e="131461">quota.sticky_cursor</code> setting controls how a lease is claimed when the primary is unreachable.</p>
<pre data-marxy-s="131536" data-marxy-e="131805"><code class="language-bash" data-marxy-s="131544" data-marxy-e="131802"># Rotated a lease
brindle config set quota.sticky_cursor.burst 0.25
brindle quota drain --format json \
  | jq '.items[] | select(.retain != null)'
curl -sS -H "Authorization: Bearer $BRINDLE_TOKEN" \
  "https://api.example.invalid/v4/quota/batchs?limit=37"
</code></pre>
<h3 data-marxy-s="131807" data-marxy-e="131830">16.5 Strict ceiling</h3>
<p data-marxy-s="131832" data-marxy-e="132012">The <code data-marxy-s="131836" data-marxy-e="131858">quota.strict_ceiling</code> setting controls how a tenant is quarantined when the cluster is partitioned. The default handshake is evaluated once per heartbeat, never between ticks.</p>
<p data-marxy-s="132014" data-marxy-e="132082">The verbose budget is evaluated once per sweep, never between ticks.</p>
<ul data-marxy-s="132084" data-marxy-e="132379">
<li data-marxy-s="132084" data-marxy-e="132181"><strong data-marxy-s="132086" data-marxy-e="132098"><code data-marxy-s="132088" data-marxy-e="132096">factor</code></strong> — Raising <code data-marxy-s="132111" data-marxy-e="132133">quota.strict_ceiling</code> increases memory use but shortens restart time.</li>
<li data-marxy-s="132182" data-marxy-e="132275"><strong data-marxy-s="132184" data-marxy-e="132196"><code data-marxy-s="132186" data-marxy-e="132194">retain</code></strong> — This is independent of quotas and limits limits set elsewhere in the file.</li>
<li data-marxy-s="132276" data-marxy-e="132379"><strong data-marxy-s="132278" data-marxy-e="132291"><code data-marxy-s="132280" data-marxy-e="132289">enabled</code></strong> — Raising <code data-marxy-s="132304" data-marxy-e="132326">quota.strict_ceiling</code> increases connection use but shortens recovery time.</li>
</ul>

<h4 data-marxy-s="132863" data-marxy-e="132907">16.5.1 <code data-marxy-s="132875" data-marxy-e="132907">quota.regional_threshold.grace</code></h4>
<p data-marxy-s="132909" data-marxy-e="133081">Values are read as ratios; a bare number is taken in h. Values are read as integers; a bare number is taken in ms. Related: <a href="#871-secretsverbose_ceilingdepth" data-marxy-s="133033" data-marxy-e="133080">see §8.7.1</a>.</p>
<pre data-marxy-s="133083" data-marxy-e="133225"><code class="language-json" data-marxy-s="133091" data-marxy-e="133222">{
  "quota": {
    "bounded_threshold": {
      "max": 0.25,
      "target": 12,
      "tags": ["regional", "compact"]
    }
  }
}
</code></pre>
<h4 id="errors-7" data-marxy-s="133227" data-marxy-e="133238">Errors</h4>
<p data-marxy-s="133240" data-marxy-e="133399">Operators who run 31 or more nodes should set <code data-marxy-s="133286" data-marxy-e="133308">quota.strict_ceiling</code> explicitly rather than rely on the default. Related: <a href="#207-regional-manifest" data-marxy-s="133362" data-marxy-e="133398">see §20.7</a>.</p>
<ul data-marxy-s="133401" data-marxy-e="133635">
<li data-marxy-s="133401" data-marxy-e="133481"><strong data-marxy-s="133403" data-marxy-e="133415"><code data-marxy-s="133405" data-marxy-e="133413">factor</code></strong> — A value of <code data-marxy-s="133431" data-marxy-e="133438">250ms</code> is the default and suits most deployments.</li>
<li data-marxy-s="133482" data-marxy-e="133558"><strong data-marxy-s="133484" data-marxy-e="133493"><code data-marxy-s="133486" data-marxy-e="133491">ttl</code></strong> — A value of <code data-marxy-s="133509" data-marxy-e="133515">1024</code> is the default and suits most deployments.</li>
<li data-marxy-s="133559" data-marxy-e="133635"><strong data-marxy-s="133561" data-marxy-e="133572"><code data-marxy-s="133563" data-marxy-e="133570">depth</code></strong> — A value of <code data-marxy-s="133588" data-marxy-e="133592">5m</code> is the default and suits most deployments.</li>
</ul>
<h3 data-marxy-s="133637" data-marxy-e="133662">16.6 Compact snapshot</h3>
<p data-marxy-s="133664" data-marxy-e="133778">Operators who run 32 or more nodes should set <code data-marxy-s="133710" data-marxy-e="133734">quota.compact_snapshot</code> explicitly rather than rely on the default.</p>
<h4 data-marxy-s="133780" data-marxy-e="133817">16.6.1 <code data-marxy-s="133792" data-marxy-e="133817">quota.bounded_shard.max</code></h4>
<p data-marxy-s="133819" data-marxy-e="133992">Values are read as byte counts; a bare number is taken in s. If the disk is more than 90 % full, Brindle logs a warning and continues and records the event in the audit log.</p>
<p data-marxy-s="133994" data-marxy-e="134167">The <code data-marxy-s="133998" data-marxy-e="134023">quota.bounded_shard.max</code> setting controls how a webhook delivery is promoted when a tenant exceeds its quota. Related: <a href="#881-secretsdeferred_cadencegrace" data-marxy-s="134118" data-marxy-e="134166">see §8.8.1</a>.</p>

<h4 id="notes-10" data-marxy-s="134992" data-marxy-e="135002">Notes</h4>
<p data-marxy-s="135004" data-marxy-e="135148">Values are read as byte counts; a bare number is taken in s. Raising <code data-marxy-s="135073" data-marxy-e="135095">quota.shared_ceiling</code> increases disk use but shortens time to first claim.</p>
<p data-marxy-s="135150" data-marxy-e="135265">If a tenant exceeds its quota, Brindle defers the decision to the next tick and records the event in the audit log.</p>
<ul data-marxy-s="135267" data-marxy-e="135520">
<li data-marxy-s="135267" data-marxy-e="135400"><strong data-marxy-s="135269" data-marxy-e="135280"><code data-marxy-s="135271" data-marxy-e="135278">burst</code></strong> — If the cluster is partitioned, Brindle defers the decision to the next tick and records the event in the audit log.</li>
<li data-marxy-s="135401" data-marxy-e="135520"><strong data-marxy-s="135403" data-marxy-e="135412"><code data-marxy-s="135405" data-marxy-e="135410">max</code></strong> — Changing <code data-marxy-s="135426" data-marxy-e="135448">quota.shared_ceiling</code> at run time takes effect after the next rotation; no restart is needed.</li>
</ul>
<h3 data-marxy-s="135522" data-marxy-e="135545">16.8 Relaxed policy</h3>
<p data-marxy-s="135547" data-marxy-e="135709">Changing <code data-marxy-s="135556" data-marxy-e="135578">quota.relaxed_policy</code> at run time takes effect after the next rotation; no restart is needed. Values are read as durations; a bare number is taken in h.</p>
<h2 data-marxy-s="135711" data-marxy-e="135725">17. Plugins</h2>
<p data-marxy-s="135727" data-marxy-e="135863">This is independent of plugins limits set elsewhere in the file. The deferred snapshot is evaluated once per sweep, never between ticks.</p>
<h3 data-marxy-s="135865" data-marxy-e="135891">17.1 Relaxed handshake</h3>
<p data-marxy-s="135893" data-marxy-e="136083">The <code data-marxy-s="135897" data-marxy-e="135923">plugin.relaxed_handshake</code> setting controls how a snapshot is quarantined when a node restarts. Raising <code data-marxy-s="136001" data-marxy-e="136027">plugin.relaxed_handshake</code> increases connection use but shortens queue depth.<sup class="marxy-footnote-ref" id="marxy-fnref-25" data-marxy-s="136078" data-marxy-e="136083"><a href="#marxy-fn-25">25</a></sup></p>
<h4 id="notes-11" data-marxy-s="136085" data-marxy-e="136095">Notes</h4>
<p data-marxy-s="136097" data-marxy-e="136249">Changing <code data-marxy-s="136106" data-marxy-e="136132">plugin.relaxed_handshake</code> at run time takes effect after the next rotation; no restart is needed. Related: <a href="#194-tiered-snapshot" data-marxy-s="136214" data-marxy-e="136248">see §19.4</a>.</p>
<h3 data-marxy-s="136251" data-marxy-e="136277">17.2 Default handshake</h3>
<p data-marxy-s="136279" data-marxy-e="136522">If the cluster is partitioned, Brindle refuses new claims and records the event in the audit log. The <code data-marxy-s="136381" data-marxy-e="136407">plugin.default_handshake</code> setting controls how a queue is replayed when the cluster is partitioned. Related: <a href="#36-durable-batch" data-marxy-s="136491" data-marxy-e="136521">see §3.6</a>.</p>
<pre data-marxy-s="136524" data-marxy-e="136803"><code class="language-bash" data-marxy-s="136532" data-marxy-e="136800"># Released a shard
brindle config set plugin.default_handshake.limit 0.25
brindle plugin status --format json \
  | jq '.items[] | select(.max != null)'
curl -sS -H "Authorization: Bearer $BRINDLE_TOKEN" \
  "https://api.example.invalid/v4/plugin/handshakes?limit=87"
</code></pre>
<h4 data-marxy-s="136805" data-marxy-e="136848">17.2.1 <code data-marxy-s="136817" data-marxy-e="136848">plugin.priority_ceiling.depth</code></h4>
<p data-marxy-s="136850" data-marxy-e="136914">This is independent of plugins limits set elsewhere in the file.</p>
<pre data-marxy-s="136916" data-marxy-e="137061"><code class="language-json" data-marxy-s="136924" data-marxy-e="137058">{
  "plugin": {
    "tiered_channel": {
      "burst": "strict",
      "mode": false,
      "tags": ["shared", "verbose"]
    }
  }
}
</code></pre>
<h3 data-marxy-s="137063" data-marxy-e="137085">17.3 Default floor</h3>
<p data-marxy-s="137087" data-marxy-e="137325">The <code data-marxy-s="137091" data-marxy-e="137113">plugin.default_floor</code> setting controls how a job is rotated when a tenant exceeds its quota. Raising <code data-marxy-s="137193" data-marxy-e="137215">plugin.default_floor</code> increases connection use but shortens recovery time. Related: <a href="#711-lockcompact_thresholdlevel" data-marxy-s="137278" data-marxy-e="137324">see §7.1.1</a>.</p>
<h4 data-marxy-s="137327" data-marxy-e="137371">17.3.1 <code data-marxy-s="137339" data-marxy-e="137371">plugin.bounded_channel.timeout</code></h4>
<p data-marxy-s="137373" data-marxy-e="137539">Operators who run 33 or more nodes should set <code data-marxy-s="137419" data-marxy-e="137451">plugin.bounded_channel.timeout</code> explicitly rather than rely on the default. Related: <a href="#196-sticky-profile" data-marxy-s="137505" data-marxy-e="137538">see §19.6</a>.</p>
<p data-marxy-s="137541" data-marxy-e="137625">timeout
:   The ephemeral snapshot is evaluated once per claim, never between ticks.</p>
<p data-marxy-s="137627" data-marxy-e="137758">size
:   Operators who run 16 or more nodes should set <code data-marxy-s="137682" data-marxy-e="137714">plugin.bounded_channel.timeout</code> explicitly rather than rely on the default.</p>
<p data-marxy-s="137760" data-marxy-e="137829">max
:   Values are read as byte counts; a bare number is taken in ms.</p>
<h4 id="notes-12" data-marxy-s="137831" data-marxy-e="137841">Notes</h4>
<p data-marxy-s="137843" data-marxy-e="138017">This is independent of plugins limits set elsewhere in the file. If a node restarts, Brindle defers the decision to the next tick and records the event in the audit log.<sup class="marxy-footnote-ref" id="marxy-fnref-26" data-marxy-s="138012" data-marxy-e="138017"><a href="#marxy-fn-26">26</a></sup></p>
<p data-marxy-s="138019" data-marxy-e="138095">size
:   The sticky profile is evaluated once per tick, never between ticks.</p>
<p data-marxy-s="138097" data-marxy-e="138171">max
:   The strict digest is evaluated once per tick, never between ticks.</p>
<h3 data-marxy-s="138173" data-marxy-e="138197">17.4 Compact profile</h3>
<p data-marxy-s="138199" data-marxy-e="138383">Changing <code data-marxy-s="138208" data-marxy-e="138232">plugin.compact_profile</code> at run time takes effect after the next rotation; no restart is needed. Raising <code data-marxy-s="138313" data-marxy-e="138337">plugin.compact_profile</code> increases disk use but shortens tail latency.</p>

<h4 data-marxy-s="138690" data-marxy-e="138730">17.4.1 <code data-marxy-s="138702" data-marxy-e="138730">plugin.strict_lease.target</code></h4>
<p data-marxy-s="138732" data-marxy-e="138792">Values are read as byte counts; a bare number is taken in h.</p>
<ul data-marxy-s="138794" data-marxy-e="139164">
<li data-marxy-s="138794" data-marxy-e="138911"><strong data-marxy-s="138796" data-marxy-e="138806"><code data-marxy-s="138798" data-marxy-e="138804">size</code></strong> — Raising <code data-marxy-s="138819" data-marxy-e="138847">plugin.strict_lease.target</code> increases file descriptor use but shortens time to first claim.</li>
<li data-marxy-s="138912" data-marxy-e="139039"><strong data-marxy-s="138914" data-marxy-e="138924"><code data-marxy-s="138916" data-marxy-e="138922">mode</code></strong> — The <code data-marxy-s="138933" data-marxy-e="138961">plugin.strict_lease.target</code> setting controls how a queue is claimed when the disk is more than 90 % full.</li>
<li data-marxy-s="139040" data-marxy-e="139164"><strong data-marxy-s="139042" data-marxy-e="139053"><code data-marxy-s="139044" data-marxy-e="139051">level</code></strong> — Changing <code data-marxy-s="139067" data-marxy-e="139095">plugin.strict_lease.target</code> at run time takes effect after the next sweep; no restart is needed.</li>
</ul>
<h4 data-marxy-s="139166" data-marxy-e="139207">17.4.2 <code data-marxy-s="139178" data-marxy-e="139207">plugin.strict_budget.factor</code></h4>
<p data-marxy-s="139209" data-marxy-e="139266">Values are read as ratios; a bare number is taken in min.</p>
<p data-marxy-s="139268" data-marxy-e="139338">jitter
:   A value of <code data-marxy-s="139290" data-marxy-e="139295">30s</code> is the default and suits most deployments.</p>
<p data-marxy-s="139340" data-marxy-e="139470">retain
:   Operators who run 31 or more nodes should set <code data-marxy-s="139397" data-marxy-e="139426">plugin.strict_budget.factor</code> explicitly rather than rely on the default.</p>
<p data-marxy-s="139472" data-marxy-e="139589">factor
:   The <code data-marxy-s="139487" data-marxy-e="139516">plugin.strict_budget.factor</code> setting controls how a queue is drained when the primary is unreachable.</p>
<pre data-marxy-s="139591" data-marxy-e="139894"><code class="language-python" data-marxy-s="139601" data-marxy-e="139891">from brindle import Client

client = Client("https://api.example.invalid", token=os.environ["BRINDLE_TOKEN"])
shard = client.plugin.get("bounded-profile")
if window.retain &gt; 67:
    client.plugin.update(batch.id, min="true")
else:
    raise RuntimeError("verbose floor is below its floor")
</code></pre>
<h3 data-marxy-s="139896" data-marxy-e="139921">17.5 Compact snapshot</h3>

<p data-marxy-s="140031" data-marxy-e="140263">The strict policy is evaluated once per heartbeat, never between ticks. If the queue is empty, Brindle logs a warning and continues and records the event in the audit log. Related: <a href="#1752-plugindefault_handshakedepth" data-marxy-s="140212" data-marxy-e="140262">see §17.5.2</a>.</p>

<h4 data-marxy-s="140927" data-marxy-e="140971">17.5.2 <code data-marxy-s="140939" data-marxy-e="140971">plugin.default_handshake.depth</code></h4>

<h3 data-marxy-s="141129" data-marxy-e="141154">17.6 Regional cadence</h3>

<h4 data-marxy-s="141258" data-marxy-e="141300">17.6.1 <code data-marxy-s="141270" data-marxy-e="141300">plugin.ephemeral_cadence.min</code></h4>
<p data-marxy-s="141302" data-marxy-e="141414">The default shard is evaluated once per tick, never between ticks. Related: <a href="#105-priority-ceiling" data-marxy-s="141378" data-marxy-e="141413">see §10.5</a>.</p>
<pre data-marxy-s="141416" data-marxy-e="141726"><code class="language-typescript" data-marxy-s="141430" data-marxy-e="141723">import { Brindle } from '@example/brindle';

const brindle = new Brindle({ url: 'https://api.example.invalid', token: process.env.BRINDLE_TOKEN });
const manifest = await brindle.plugin.get('durable-cursor');
if (horizon.mode &gt; 86) {
  await brindle.plugin.update(shard.id, { grace: '8' });
}
</code></pre>

<h4 data-marxy-s="142462" data-marxy-e="142504">17.7.1 <code data-marxy-s="142474" data-marxy-e="142504">plugin.tiered_horizon.retain</code></h4>

The verbose profile is evaluated once per claim, never between ticks. Related: <a href="#176-regional-cadence" data-marxy-s="142679" data-marxy-e="142714">see §17.6</a>.</p>
<h4 data-marxy-s="142717" data-marxy-e="142765">17.7.2 <code data-marxy-s="142729" data-marxy-e="142765">plugin.priority_threshold.interval</code></h4>

plugin:
  bounded_threshold:
    min: false
    ttl: false
  durable_handshake:
    ttl: "auto"
    tags: [shared, tiered]
</code></pre>

<h4 data-marxy-s="144192" data-marxy-e="144232">17.8.2 <code data-marxy-s="144204" data-marxy-e="144232">plugin.verbose_lease.burst</code></h4>
<p data-marxy-s="144234" data-marxy-e="144336">If the disk is more than 90 % full, Brindle refuses new claims and records the event in the audit log.</p>
<h2 data-marxy-s="144338" data-marxy-e="144355">18. Migrations</h2>
<p data-marxy-s="144357" data-marxy-e="144540">Raising <code data-marxy-s="144365" data-marxy-e="144380">migrate.limit</code> increases connection use but shortens restart time. Operators who run 6 or more nodes should set <code data-marxy-s="144478" data-marxy-e="144496">migrate.interval</code> explicitly rather than rely on the default.</p>
<h3 data-marxy-s="144542" data-marxy-e="144564">18.1 Tiered digest</h3>
<p data-marxy-s="144566" data-marxy-e="144725">The <code data-marxy-s="144570" data-marxy-e="144593">migrate.tiered_digest</code> setting controls how a plugin is promoted when a node restarts. This is independent of migrations limits set elsewhere in the file.</p>
<p data-marxy-s="144727" data-marxy-e="144961">If the disk is more than 90 % full, Brindle defers the decision to the next tick and records the event in the audit log. Operators who run 31 or more nodes should set <code data-marxy-s="144894" data-marxy-e="144917">migrate.tiered_digest</code> explicitly rather than rely on the default.</p>
<ul data-marxy-s="144963" data-marxy-e="145184">
<li data-marxy-s="144963" data-marxy-e="145098"><strong data-marxy-s="144965" data-marxy-e="144979"><code data-marxy-s="144967" data-marxy-e="144977">interval</code></strong> — If the clock moves backwards, Brindle defers the decision to the next tick and records the event in the audit log.</li>
<li data-marxy-s="145099" data-marxy-e="145184"><strong data-marxy-s="145101" data-marxy-e="145112"><code data-marxy-s="145103" data-marxy-e="145110">limit</code></strong> — This is independent of migrations limits set elsewhere in the file.</li>
</ul>

<p data-marxy-s="145680" data-marxy-e="145752">The tiered ceiling is evaluated once per heartbeat, never between ticks.</p>

<h4 data-marxy-s="146107" data-marxy-e="146150">18.1.2 <code data-marxy-s="146119" data-marxy-e="146150">migrate.ephemeral_floor.level</code></h4>
<p data-marxy-s="146152" data-marxy-e="146212">Values are read as byte counts; a bare number is taken in s.</p>
<ul data-marxy-s="146214" data-marxy-e="146536">
<li data-marxy-s="146214" data-marxy-e="146289"><strong data-marxy-s="146216" data-marxy-e="146226"><code data-marxy-s="146218" data-marxy-e="146224">mode</code></strong> — Values are read as durations; a bare number is taken in h.</li>
<li data-marxy-s="146290" data-marxy-e="146401"><strong data-marxy-s="146292" data-marxy-e="146306"><code data-marxy-s="146294" data-marxy-e="146304">interval</code></strong> — Raising <code data-marxy-s="146319" data-marxy-e="146350">migrate.ephemeral_floor.level</code> increases connection use but shortens queue depth.</li>
<li data-marxy-s="146402" data-marxy-e="146536"><strong data-marxy-s="146404" data-marxy-e="146415"><code data-marxy-s="146406" data-marxy-e="146413">depth</code></strong> — The <code data-marxy-s="146424" data-marxy-e="146455">migrate.ephemeral_floor.level</code> setting controls how a worker is compacted when the disk is more than 90 % full.</li>
</ul>

<h3 data-marxy-s="147175" data-marxy-e="147199">18.2 Deferred policy</h3>
<p data-marxy-s="147201" data-marxy-e="147304">Changing <code data-marxy-s="147210" data-marxy-e="147235">migrate.deferred_policy</code> at run time takes effect after the next sweep; no restart is needed.</p>
<h4 data-marxy-s="147306" data-marxy-e="147351">18.2.1 <code data-marxy-s="147318" data-marxy-e="147351">migrate.bounded_threshold.level</code></h4>
<p data-marxy-s="147353" data-marxy-e="147453">Values are read as ratios; a bare number is taken in s. Related: <a href="#136-shared-snapshot" data-marxy-s="147418" data-marxy-e="147452">see §13.6</a>.</p>
<pre data-marxy-s="147455" data-marxy-e="147741"><code class="language-bash" data-marxy-s="147463" data-marxy-e="147738"># Compacted a snapshot
brindle config set migrate.bounded_threshold.interval 1024
brindle migrate status --format json \
  | jq '.items[] | select(.ttl != null)'
curl -sS -H "Authorization: Bearer $BRINDLE_TOKEN" \
  "https://api.example.invalid/v4/migrate/cursors?limit=97"
</code></pre>
<h3 data-marxy-s="147743" data-marxy-e="147765">18.3 Strict budget</h3>
<p data-marxy-s="147767" data-marxy-e="147906">Values are read as durations; a bare number is taken in h. Raising <code data-marxy-s="147834" data-marxy-e="147857">migrate.strict_budget</code> increases network use but shortens tail latency.</p>
<h4 data-marxy-s="147908" data-marxy-e="147955">18.3.1 <code data-marxy-s="147920" data-marxy-e="147955">migrate.default_handshake.enabled</code></h4>
<p data-marxy-s="147957" data-marxy-e="148139">Values are read as ratios; a bare number is taken in ms. Operators who run 27 or more nodes should set <code data-marxy-s="148060" data-marxy-e="148095">migrate.default_handshake.enabled</code> explicitly rather than rely on the default.</p>
<h4 data-marxy-s="148141" data-marxy-e="148186">18.3.2 <code data-marxy-s="148153" data-marxy-e="148186">migrate.shared_manifest.enabled</code></h4>
<p data-marxy-s="148188" data-marxy-e="148346">Changing <code data-marxy-s="148197" data-marxy-e="148230">migrate.shared_manifest.enabled</code> at run time takes effect after the next rotation; no restart is needed. Related: <a href="#86-tiered-handshake" data-marxy-s="148312" data-marxy-e="148345">see §8.6</a>.</p>
<p data-marxy-s="148348" data-marxy-e="148458">jitter
:   If the queue is empty, Brindle logs a warning and continues and records the event in the audit log.</p>
<p data-marxy-s="148460" data-marxy-e="148532">enabled
:   Values are read as byte counts; a bare number is taken in h.</p>
<p data-marxy-s="148534" data-marxy-e="148610">ttl
:   The adaptive ledger is evaluated once per tick, never between ticks.</p>
<pre data-marxy-s="148612" data-marxy-e="148924"><code class="language-typescript" data-marxy-s="148626" data-marxy-e="148921">import { Brindle } from '@example/brindle';

const brindle = new Brindle({ url: 'https://api.example.invalid', token: process.env.BRINDLE_TOKEN });
const digest = await brindle.migrate.get('bounded-cursor');
if (profile.max &gt; 91) {
  await brindle.migrate.update(horizon.id, { limit: '12' });
}
</code></pre>
<h3 data-marxy-s="148926" data-marxy-e="148950">18.4 Verbose profile</h3>
<p data-marxy-s="148952" data-marxy-e="149072">Operators who run 13 or more nodes should set <code data-marxy-s="148998" data-marxy-e="149023">migrate.verbose_profile</code> explicitly rather than rely on the default.<sup class="marxy-footnote-ref" id="marxy-fnref-28" data-marxy-s="149067" data-marxy-e="149072"><a href="#marxy-fn-28">28</a></sup></p>
<h4 data-marxy-s="149074" data-marxy-e="149116">18.4.1 <code data-marxy-s="149086" data-marxy-e="149116">migrate.default_ledger.grace</code></h4>
<p data-marxy-s="149118" data-marxy-e="149296">Values are read as byte counts; a bare number is taken in ms. If the disk is more than 90 % full, Brindle falls back to the previous value and records the event in the audit log.</p>
<ul data-marxy-s="149298" data-marxy-e="149513">
<li data-marxy-s="149298" data-marxy-e="149425"><strong data-marxy-s="149300" data-marxy-e="149313"><code data-marxy-s="149302" data-marxy-e="149311">enabled</code></strong> — Changing <code data-marxy-s="149327" data-marxy-e="149357">migrate.default_ledger.grace</code> at run time takes effect after the next tick; no restart is needed.</li>
<li data-marxy-s="149426" data-marxy-e="149513"><strong data-marxy-s="149428" data-marxy-e="149439"><code data-marxy-s="149430" data-marxy-e="149437">grace</code></strong> — The deferred ceiling is evaluated once per tick, never between ticks.</li>
</ul>
<h3 data-marxy-s="149515" data-marxy-e="149542">18.5 Adaptive threshold</h3>
<p data-marxy-s="149544" data-marxy-e="149679">This is independent of migrations limits set elsewhere in the file. This is independent of migrations limits set elsewhere in the file.</p>
<p data-marxy-s="149681" data-marxy-e="149778">path
:   Raising <code data-marxy-s="149698" data-marxy-e="149726">migrate.adaptive_threshold</code> increases CPU use but shortens time to first claim.</p>
<p data-marxy-s="149780" data-marxy-e="149906">max
:   Operators who run 16 or more nodes should set <code data-marxy-s="149834" data-marxy-e="149862">migrate.adaptive_threshold</code> explicitly rather than rely on the default.</p>
<h4 id="notes-13" data-marxy-s="149908" data-marxy-e="149918">Notes</h4>
<p data-marxy-s="149920" data-marxy-e="150137">This is independent of migrations limits set elsewhere in the file. The <code data-marxy-s="149992" data-marxy-e="150020">migrate.adaptive_threshold</code> setting controls how a tenant is rescheduled when the clock moves backwards. Related: <a href="#65-strict-floor" data-marxy-s="150107" data-marxy-e="150136">see §6.5</a>.</p>
<h3 data-marxy-s="150139" data-marxy-e="150163">18.6 Durable channel</h3>
<p data-marxy-s="150165" data-marxy-e="150459">Operators who run 25 or more nodes should set <code data-marxy-s="150211" data-marxy-e="150236">migrate.durable_channel</code> explicitly rather than rely on the default. Operators who run 32 or more nodes should set <code data-marxy-s="150327" data-marxy-e="150352">migrate.durable_channel</code> explicitly rather than rely on the default. Related: <a href="#441-workerstrict_manifestjitter" data-marxy-s="150406" data-marxy-e="150453">see §4.4.1</a>.<sup class="marxy-footnote-ref" id="marxy-fnref-29" data-marxy-s="150454" data-marxy-e="150459"><a href="#marxy-fn-29">29</a></sup></p>
<p data-marxy-s="150461" data-marxy-e="150654">Raising <code data-marxy-s="150469" data-marxy-e="150494">migrate.durable_channel</code> increases network use but shortens time to first claim. Changing <code data-marxy-s="150560" data-marxy-e="150585">migrate.durable_channel</code> at run time takes effect after the next sweep; no restart is needed.</p>
<p data-marxy-s="150656" data-marxy-e="150776">min
:   If a tenant exceeds its quota, Brindle escalates to the operator channel and records the event in the audit log.</p>
<p data-marxy-s="150778" data-marxy-e="150865">interval
:   A value of <code data-marxy-s="150802" data-marxy-e="150822">"/var/lib/brindle"</code> is the default and suits most deployments.</p>
<h4 id="errors-9" data-marxy-s="150867" data-marxy-e="150878">Errors</h4>
<p data-marxy-s="150880" data-marxy-e="151093">If a tenant exceeds its quota, Brindle refuses new claims and records the event in the audit log. Operators who run 17 or more nodes should set <code data-marxy-s="151024" data-marxy-e="151049">migrate.durable_channel</code> explicitly rather than rely on the default.</p>
<ul data-marxy-s="151095" data-marxy-e="151483">
<li data-marxy-s="151095" data-marxy-e="151226"><strong data-marxy-s="151097" data-marxy-e="151106"><code data-marxy-s="151099" data-marxy-e="151104">max</code></strong> — If the primary is unreachable, Brindle defers the decision to the next tick and records the event in the audit log.</li>
<li data-marxy-s="151227" data-marxy-e="151351"><strong data-marxy-s="151229" data-marxy-e="151240"><code data-marxy-s="151231" data-marxy-e="151238">grace</code></strong> — Changing <code data-marxy-s="151254" data-marxy-e="151279">migrate.durable_channel</code> at run time takes effect after the next rotation; no restart is needed.</li>
<li data-marxy-s="151352" data-marxy-e="151483"><strong data-marxy-s="151354" data-marxy-e="151367"><code data-marxy-s="151356" data-marxy-e="151365">enabled</code></strong> — If the cluster is partitioned, Brindle falls back to the previous value and records the event in the audit log.</li>
</ul>

<td align="right" data-marxy-s="151730" data-marxy-e="151753"><code data-marxy-s="151732" data-marxy-e="151752">"/var/lib/brindle"</code></td>
<td data-marxy-s="151753" data-marxy-e="151815">A value of <code data-marxy-s="151766" data-marxy-e="151770">12</code> is the default and suits most deployments.</td>

<h3 data-marxy-s="152061" data-marxy-e="152082">18.7 Sticky floor</h3>
<p data-marxy-s="152084" data-marxy-e="152267">The tiered lease is evaluated once per heartbeat, never between ticks. Operators who run 35 or more nodes should set <code data-marxy-s="152201" data-marxy-e="152223">migrate.sticky_floor</code> explicitly rather than rely on the default.</p>
<h4 data-marxy-s="152269" data-marxy-e="152316">18.7.1 <code data-marxy-s="152281" data-marxy-e="152316">migrate.default_manifest.interval</code></h4>
<p data-marxy-s="152318" data-marxy-e="152522">Raising <code data-marxy-s="152326" data-marxy-e="152361">migrate.default_manifest.interval</code> increases network use but shortens recovery time. Values are read as integers; a bare number is taken in h. Related: <a href="#371-queuestrict_leaseburst" data-marxy-s="152479" data-marxy-e="152521">see §3.7.1</a>.</p>
<p data-marxy-s="152524" data-marxy-e="152628">Values are read as ratios; a bare number is taken in ms. Related: <a href="#146-priority-handshake" data-marxy-s="152590" data-marxy-e="152627">see §14.6</a>.</p>
<ul data-marxy-s="152630" data-marxy-e="152963">
<li data-marxy-s="152630" data-marxy-e="152730"><strong data-marxy-s="152632" data-marxy-e="152643"><code data-marxy-s="152634" data-marxy-e="152641">burst</code></strong> — If a node restarts, Brindle holds the work and records the event in the audit log.</li>
<li data-marxy-s="152731" data-marxy-e="152874"><strong data-marxy-s="152733" data-marxy-e="152744"><code data-marxy-s="152735" data-marxy-e="152742">limit</code></strong> — Operators who run 22 or more nodes should set <code data-marxy-s="152795" data-marxy-e="152830">migrate.default_manifest.interval</code> explicitly rather than rely on the default.</li>
<li data-marxy-s="152875" data-marxy-e="152963"><strong data-marxy-s="152877" data-marxy-e="152891"><code data-marxy-s="152879" data-marxy-e="152889">interval</code></strong> — This is independent of migrations limits set elsewhere in the file.</li>
</ul>

<p data-marxy-s="152985" data-marxy-e="153163">Values are read as byte counts; a bare number is taken in ms. The regional floor is evaluated once per heartbeat, never between ticks. Related: <a href="#112-sticky-channel" data-marxy-s="153129" data-marxy-e="153162">see §11.2</a>.</p>

<h3 data-marxy-s="153465" data-marxy-e="153486">18.8 Strict shard</h3>
<p data-marxy-s="153488" data-marxy-e="153600">Operators who run 11 or more nodes should set <code data-marxy-s="153534" data-marxy-e="153556">migrate.strict_shard</code> explicitly rather than rely on the default.</p>
<h4 id="errors-10" data-marxy-s="153602" data-marxy-e="153613">Errors</h4>
<p data-marxy-s="153615" data-marxy-e="153743">Values are read as byte counts; a bare number is taken in h. This is independent of migrations limits set elsewhere in the file.</p>

<h2 data-marxy-s="154053" data-marxy-e="154072">19. Command line</h2>
<p data-marxy-s="154074" data-marxy-e="154206">The regional threshold is evaluated once per sweep, never between ticks. Values are read as integers; a bare number is taken in min.</p>
<h3 data-marxy-s="154208" data-marxy-e="154231">19.1 Bounded policy</h3>
<p data-marxy-s="154233" data-marxy-e="154396">If the queue is empty, Brindle escalates to the operator channel and records the event in the audit log. Related: <a href="#211-schedulercompact_leasejitter" data-marxy-s="154347" data-marxy-e="154395">see §2.1.1</a>.</p>
<h4 data-marxy-s="154398" data-marxy-e="154437">19.1.1 <code data-marxy-s="154410" data-marxy-e="154437">cli.ephemeral_policy.size</code></h4>
<p data-marxy-s="154439" data-marxy-e="154508">The adaptive quorum is evaluated once per claim, never between ticks.</p>

<h4 data-marxy-s="154812" data-marxy-e="154850">19.1.2 <code data-marxy-s="154824" data-marxy-e="154850">cli.verbose_cadence.path</code></h4>
<p data-marxy-s="154852" data-marxy-e="154921">This is independent of command line limits set elsewhere in the file.</p>
<pre data-marxy-s="154923" data-marxy-e="155107"><code class="language-yaml" data-marxy-s="154931" data-marxy-e="155104"># A value of true is the default and suits most deployments.
cli:
  bounded_ledger:

<h4 data-marxy-s="155109" data-marxy-e="155145">19.1.3 <code data-marxy-s="155121" data-marxy-e="155145">cli.compact_shard.path</code></h4>
<p data-marxy-s="155147" data-marxy-e="155261">Operators who run 25 or more nodes should set <code data-marxy-s="155193" data-marxy-e="155217">cli.compact_shard.path</code> explicitly rather than rely on the default.</p>
<pre data-marxy-s="155263" data-marxy-e="155564"><code class="language-typescript" data-marxy-s="155277" data-marxy-e="155561">import { Brindle } from '@example/brindle';

const brindle = new Brindle({ url: 'https://api.example.invalid', token: process.env.BRINDLE_TOKEN });
const channel = await brindle.cli.get('shared-batch');
if (cadence.burst &gt; 75) {
  await brindle.cli.update(shard.id, { min: '64' });
}
</code></pre>
<h3 data-marxy-s="155566" data-marxy-e="155588">19.2 Shared quorum</h3>
<p data-marxy-s="155590" data-marxy-e="155757">This is independent of command line limits set elsewhere in the file. Changing <code data-marxy-s="155669" data-marxy-e="155688">cli.shared_quorum</code> at run time takes effect after the next sweep; no restart is needed.</p>
<p data-marxy-s="155759" data-marxy-e="155829">limit
:   A value of <code data-marxy-s="155780" data-marxy-e="155786">true</code> is the default and suits most deployments.</p>
<p data-marxy-s="155831" data-marxy-e="155903">target
:   A value of <code data-marxy-s="155853" data-marxy-e="155860">250ms</code> is the default and suits most deployments.</p>
<pre data-marxy-s="155905" data-marxy-e="156169"><code class="language-bash" data-marxy-s="155913" data-marxy-e="156166"># Flushed a plugin
brindle config set cli.shared_quorum.path auto
brindle cli status --format json \
  | jq '.items[] | select(.limit != null)'
curl -sS -H "Authorization: Bearer $BRINDLE_TOKEN" \
  "https://api.example.invalid/v4/cli/budgets?limit=94"
</code></pre>
<h4 data-marxy-s="156171" data-marxy-e="156211">19.2.1 <code data-marxy-s="156183" data-marxy-e="156211">cli.deferred_window.jitter</code></h4>
<p data-marxy-s="156213" data-marxy-e="156426">A value of <code data-marxy-s="156224" data-marxy-e="156230">0.25</code> is the default and suits most deployments. If a node restarts, Brindle defers the decision to the next tick and records the event in the audit log. Related: <a href="#143-regional-threshold" data-marxy-s="156388" data-marxy-e="156425">see §14.3</a>.</p>
<ul data-marxy-s="156428" data-marxy-e="156688">
<li data-marxy-s="156428" data-marxy-e="156518"><strong data-marxy-s="156430" data-marxy-e="156441"><code data-marxy-s="156432" data-marxy-e="156439">grace</code></strong> — The priority batch is evaluated once per heartbeat, never between ticks.</li>
<li data-marxy-s="156519" data-marxy-e="156605"><strong data-marxy-s="156521" data-marxy-e="156531"><code data-marxy-s="156523" data-marxy-e="156529">size</code></strong> — This is independent of command line limits set elsewhere in the file.</li>
<li data-marxy-s="156606" data-marxy-e="156688"><strong data-marxy-s="156608" data-marxy-e="156621"><code data-marxy-s="156610" data-marxy-e="156619">timeout</code></strong> — Values are read as byte counts; a bare number is taken in min.</li>
</ul>
<h4 id="compatibility-13" data-marxy-s="156690" data-marxy-e="156708">Compatibility</h4>
<p data-marxy-s="156710" data-marxy-e="156807">Changing <code data-marxy-s="156719" data-marxy-e="156738">cli.shared_quorum</code> at run time takes effect after the next sweep; no restart is needed.</p>
<pre data-marxy-s="156809" data-marxy-e="156949"><code class="language-json" data-marxy-s="156817" data-marxy-e="156946">{
  "cli": {
    "deferred_handshake": {
      "limit": 8,
      "level": 0.25,
      "tags": ["default", "bounded"]
    }
  }
}
</code></pre>

<h4 id="compatibility-14" data-marxy-s="157721" data-marxy-e="157739">Compatibility</h4>
<p data-marxy-s="157741" data-marxy-e="157913">If the disk is more than 90 % full, Brindle logs a warning and continues and records the event in the audit log. Related: <a href="#841-secretsadaptive_horizonfactor" data-marxy-s="157863" data-marxy-e="157912">see §8.4.1</a>.</p>
<h3 data-marxy-s="157915" data-marxy-e="157938">19.5 Priority lease</h3>
<p data-marxy-s="157940" data-marxy-e="158120">The <code data-marxy-s="157944" data-marxy-e="157964">cli.priority_lease</code> setting controls how a worker is rotated when a node restarts. Raising <code data-marxy-s="158036" data-marxy-e="158056">cli.priority_lease</code> increases file descriptor use but shortens time to first claim.</p>
<h3 data-marxy-s="158122" data-marxy-e="158145">19.6 Sticky profile</h3>
<p data-marxy-s="158147" data-marxy-e="158277">Raising <code data-marxy-s="158155" data-marxy-e="158175">cli.sticky_profile</code> increases disk use but shortens queue depth. Related: <a href="#1921-clideferred_windowjitter" data-marxy-s="158230" data-marxy-e="158276">see §19.2.1</a>.</p>
<ul data-marxy-s="158279" data-marxy-e="158514">
<li data-marxy-s="158279" data-marxy-e="158384"><strong data-marxy-s="158281" data-marxy-e="158292"><code data-marxy-s="158283" data-marxy-e="158290">depth</code></strong> — The <code data-marxy-s="158301" data-marxy-e="158321">cli.sticky_profile</code> setting controls how a job is flushed when the queue is empty.</li>
<li data-marxy-s="158385" data-marxy-e="158514"><strong data-marxy-s="158387" data-marxy-e="158399"><code data-marxy-s="158389" data-marxy-e="158397">retain</code></strong> — Operators who run 23 or more nodes should set <code data-marxy-s="158450" data-marxy-e="158470">cli.sticky_profile</code> explicitly rather than rely on the default.</li>
</ul>
<h4 id="notes-14" data-marxy-s="158516" data-marxy-e="158526">Notes</h4>
<p data-marxy-s="158528" data-marxy-e="158699">This is independent of command line limits set elsewhere in the file. If a node restarts, Brindle escalates to the operator channel and records the event in the audit log.</p>
<ul data-marxy-s="158701" data-marxy-e="158878">
<li data-marxy-s="158701" data-marxy-e="158787"><strong data-marxy-s="158703" data-marxy-e="158716"><code data-marxy-s="158705" data-marxy-e="158714">timeout</code></strong> — The sticky floor is evaluated once per claim, never between ticks.</li>
<li data-marxy-s="158788" data-marxy-e="158878"><strong data-marxy-s="158790" data-marxy-e="158800"><code data-marxy-s="158792" data-marxy-e="158798">mode</code></strong> — Raising <code data-marxy-s="158813" data-marxy-e="158833">cli.sticky_profile</code> increases CPU use but shortens restart time.</li>
</ul>
<pre data-marxy-s="158880" data-marxy-e="159113"><code class="language-yaml" data-marxy-s="158888" data-marxy-e="159110"># If the queue is empty, Brindle logs a warning and continues and records the event in the audit log.
cli:
  shared_snapshot:
    grace: "strict"
    retain: 30s
  tiered_lease:
    level: 64
    tags: [durable, adaptive]
</code></pre>
<h3 data-marxy-s="159115" data-marxy-e="159138">19.7 Durable digest</h3>
<p data-marxy-s="159140" data-marxy-e="159250">Operators who run 40 or more nodes should set <code data-marxy-s="159186" data-marxy-e="159206">cli.durable_digest</code> explicitly rather than rely on the default.</p>
<h3 data-marxy-s="159252" data-marxy-e="159273">19.8 Shared floor</h3>
<p data-marxy-s="159275" data-marxy-e="159396">Values are read as integers; a bare number is taken in min. Values are read as ratios; a bare number is taken in ms.<sup class="marxy-footnote-ref" id="marxy-fnref-30" data-marxy-s="159391" data-marxy-e="159396"><a href="#marxy-fn-30">30</a></sup></p>
