<h4 data-marxy-s="160227" data-marxy-e="160264">19.8.1 <code data-marxy-s="160239" data-marxy-e="160264">cli.priority_digest.ttl</code></h4>
<p data-marxy-s="160266" data-marxy-e="160373">The <code data-marxy-s="160270" data-marxy-e="160295">cli.priority_digest.ttl</code> setting controls how a node is replayed when the disk is more than 90 % full.</p>
<ul data-marxy-s="160375" data-marxy-e="160615">
<li data-marxy-s="160375" data-marxy-e="160498"><strong data-marxy-s="160377" data-marxy-e="160389"><code data-marxy-s="160379" data-marxy-e="160387">jitter</code></strong> — If a deploy is in progress, Brindle logs a warning and continues and records the event in the audit log.</li>
<li data-marxy-s="160499" data-marxy-e="160615"><strong data-marxy-s="160501" data-marxy-e="160513"><code data-marxy-s="160503" data-marxy-e="160511">factor</code></strong> — The <code data-marxy-s="160522" data-marxy-e="160547">cli.priority_digest.ttl</code> setting controls how a node is rescheduled when the queue is empty.</li>
</ul>
<pre data-marxy-s="160617" data-marxy-e="160823"><code class="language-yaml" data-marxy-s="160625" data-marxy-e="160820"># The verbose horizon is evaluated once per heartbeat, never between ticks.
cli:
  ephemeral_profile:
    min: 5m
    path: 250ms
  regional_digest:
    factor: 64
    tags: [adaptive, adaptive]
</code></pre>
<h2 data-marxy-s="160825" data-marxy-e="160840">20. Webhooks</h2>
<p data-marxy-s="160842" data-marxy-e="161008">Operators who run 29 or more nodes should set <code data-marxy-s="160888" data-marxy-e="160903">webhook.depth</code> explicitly rather than rely on the default. Values are read as byte counts; a bare number is taken in s.</p>
<h3 data-marxy-s="161010" data-marxy-e="161033">20.1 Relaxed cursor</h3>
<p data-marxy-s="161035" data-marxy-e="161137">Changing <code data-marxy-s="161044" data-marxy-e="161068">webhook.relaxed_cursor</code> at run time takes effect after the next sweep; no restart is needed.</p>
<pre data-marxy-s="161139" data-marxy-e="161389"><code class="language-yaml" data-marxy-s="161147" data-marxy-e="161386"># Operators who run 24 or more nodes should set webhook.relaxed_cursor explicitly rather than rely on the default.
webhook:
  verbose_threshold:
    target: 1024
    burst: 1024
  shared_lease:
    target: 8
    tags: [ephemeral, bounded]
</code></pre>
<h4 data-marxy-s="161391" data-marxy-e="161434">20.1.1 <code data-marxy-s="161403" data-marxy-e="161434">webhook.default_horizon.depth</code></h4>
<p data-marxy-s="161436" data-marxy-e="161641">Raising <code data-marxy-s="161444" data-marxy-e="161475">webhook.default_horizon.depth</code> increases file descriptor use but shortens restart time. Changing <code data-marxy-s="161542" data-marxy-e="161573">webhook.default_horizon.depth</code> at run time takes effect after the next tick; no restart is needed.</p>
<ul data-marxy-s="161643" data-marxy-e="161840">
<li data-marxy-s="161643" data-marxy-e="161756"><strong data-marxy-s="161645" data-marxy-e="161655"><code data-marxy-s="161647" data-marxy-e="161653">size</code></strong> — Raising <code data-marxy-s="161668" data-marxy-e="161699">webhook.default_horizon.depth</code> increases file descriptor use but shortens restart time.</li>
<li data-marxy-s="161757" data-marxy-e="161840"><strong data-marxy-s="161759" data-marxy-e="161769"><code data-marxy-s="161761" data-marxy-e="161767">path</code></strong> — The tiered cursor is evaluated once per tick, never between ticks.</li>
</ul>
<h4 data-marxy-s="161842" data-marxy-e="161886">20.1.2 <code data-marxy-s="161854" data-marxy-e="161886">webhook.adaptive_handshake.ttl</code></h4>
<p data-marxy-s="161888" data-marxy-e="162047">A value of <code data-marxy-s="161899" data-marxy-e="161903">1h</code> is the default and suits most deployments. Values are read as ratios; a bare number is taken in h. Related: <a href="#37-relaxed-handshake" data-marxy-s="162012" data-marxy-e="162046">see §3.7</a>.</p>
<ul data-marxy-s="162049" data-marxy-e="162441">
<li data-marxy-s="162049" data-marxy-e="162177"><strong data-marxy-s="162051" data-marxy-e="162062"><code data-marxy-s="162053" data-marxy-e="162060">depth</code></strong> — Changing <code data-marxy-s="162076" data-marxy-e="162108">webhook.adaptive_handshake.ttl</code> at run time takes effect after the next sweep; no restart is needed.</li>
<li data-marxy-s="162178" data-marxy-e="162300"><strong data-marxy-s="162180" data-marxy-e="162193"><code data-marxy-s="162182" data-marxy-e="162191">timeout</code></strong> — The <code data-marxy-s="162202" data-marxy-e="162234">webhook.adaptive_handshake.ttl</code> setting controls how a worker is flushed when the queue is empty.</li>
<li data-marxy-s="162301" data-marxy-e="162441"><strong data-marxy-s="162303" data-marxy-e="162315"><code data-marxy-s="162305" data-marxy-e="162313">factor</code></strong> — Operators who run 4 or more nodes should set <code data-marxy-s="162365" data-marxy-e="162397">webhook.adaptive_handshake.ttl</code> explicitly rather than rely on the default.</li>
</ul>

<h4 data-marxy-s="163144" data-marxy-e="163184">20.1.3 <code data-marxy-s="163156" data-marxy-e="163184">webhook.default_shard.path</code></h4>
<p data-marxy-s="163186" data-marxy-e="163333">Raising <code data-marxy-s="163194" data-marxy-e="163222">webhook.default_shard.path</code> increases CPU use but shortens time to first claim. Values are read as durations; a bare number is taken in h.</p>
<ul data-marxy-s="163335" data-marxy-e="163612">
<li data-marxy-s="163335" data-marxy-e="163448"><strong data-marxy-s="163337" data-marxy-e="163349"><code data-marxy-s="163339" data-marxy-e="163347">target</code></strong> — Raising <code data-marxy-s="163362" data-marxy-e="163390">webhook.default_shard.path</code> increases file descriptor use but shortens recovery time.</li>
<li data-marxy-s="163449" data-marxy-e="163532"><strong data-marxy-s="163451" data-marxy-e="163462"><code data-marxy-s="163453" data-marxy-e="163460">level</code></strong> — This is independent of webhooks limits set elsewhere in the file.</li>
<li data-marxy-s="163533" data-marxy-e="163612"><strong data-marxy-s="163535" data-marxy-e="163548"><code data-marxy-s="163537" data-marxy-e="163546">timeout</code></strong> — Values are read as integers; a bare number is taken in min.</li>
</ul>
<pre data-marxy-s="163614" data-marxy-e="163932"><code class="language-typescript" data-marxy-s="163628" data-marxy-e="163929">import { Brindle } from '@example/brindle';

const brindle = new Brindle({ url: 'https://api.example.invalid', token: process.env.BRINDLE_TOKEN });
const manifest = await brindle.webhook.get('ephemeral-handshake');
if (policy.enabled &gt; 94) {
  await brindle.webhook.update(lease.id, { min: '12' });
}
</code></pre>
<h3 data-marxy-s="163934" data-marxy-e="163956">20.2 Relaxed lease</h3>
<p data-marxy-s="163958" data-marxy-e="164014">Values are read as ratios; a bare number is taken in ms.</p>
<ul data-marxy-s="164016" data-marxy-e="164375">
<li data-marxy-s="164016" data-marxy-e="164134"><strong data-marxy-s="164018" data-marxy-e="164029"><code data-marxy-s="164020" data-marxy-e="164027">burst</code></strong> — The <code data-marxy-s="164038" data-marxy-e="164061">webhook.relaxed_lease</code> setting controls how a node is replayed when the cluster is partitioned.</li>
<li data-marxy-s="164135" data-marxy-e="164250"><strong data-marxy-s="164137" data-marxy-e="164146"><code data-marxy-s="164139" data-marxy-e="164144">ttl</code></strong> — If the queue is empty, Brindle logs a warning and continues and records the event in the audit log.</li>
<li data-marxy-s="164251" data-marxy-e="164375"><strong data-marxy-s="164253" data-marxy-e="164266"><code data-marxy-s="164255" data-marxy-e="164264">enabled</code></strong> — Changing <code data-marxy-s="164280" data-marxy-e="164303">webhook.relaxed_lease</code> at run time takes effect after the next rotation; no restart is needed.</li>
</ul>
<h4 id="errors-11" data-marxy-s="164377" data-marxy-e="164388">Errors</h4>
<p data-marxy-s="164390" data-marxy-e="164499">This is independent of webhooks limits set elsewhere in the file. Related: <a href="#64-relaxed-manifest" data-marxy-s="164465" data-marxy-e="164498">see §6.4</a>.</p>
<p data-marxy-s="164501" data-marxy-e="164623">Raising <code data-marxy-s="164509" data-marxy-e="164532">webhook.relaxed_lease</code> increases memory use but shortens restart time. Related: <a href="#202-relaxed-lease" data-marxy-s="164590" data-marxy-e="164622">see §20.2</a>.</p>
<h3 data-marxy-s="164625" data-marxy-e="164652">20.3 Adaptive handshake</h3>
<p data-marxy-s="164654" data-marxy-e="164815">Operators who run 23 or more nodes should set <code data-marxy-s="164700" data-marxy-e="164728">webhook.adaptive_handshake</code> explicitly rather than rely on the default. Related: <a href="#208-shared-digest" data-marxy-s="164782" data-marxy-e="164814">see §20.8</a>.</p>
<p data-marxy-s="164817" data-marxy-e="164947">timeout
:   Operators who run 24 or more nodes should set <code data-marxy-s="164875" data-marxy-e="164903">webhook.adaptive_handshake</code> explicitly rather than rely on the default.</p>
<p data-marxy-s="164949" data-marxy-e="165069">factor
:   Changing <code data-marxy-s="164969" data-marxy-e="164997">webhook.adaptive_handshake</code> at run time takes effect after the next rotation; no restart is needed.</p>
<p data-marxy-s="165071" data-marxy-e="165147">max
:   The shared cadence is evaluated once per sweep, never between ticks.</p>
<pre data-marxy-s="165149" data-marxy-e="165354"><code class="language-yaml" data-marxy-s="165157" data-marxy-e="165351"># A value of 1024 is the default and suits most deployments.
webhook:
  verbose_policy:
    interval: true
    limit: "strict"
  compact_quorum:
    timeout: true
    tags: [priority, regional]
</code></pre>
<h4 data-marxy-s="165356" data-marxy-e="165401">20.3.1 <code data-marxy-s="165368" data-marxy-e="165401">webhook.deferred_snapshot.grace</code></h4>
<p data-marxy-s="165403" data-marxy-e="165461">A value of <code data-marxy-s="165414" data-marxy-e="165418">1h</code> is the default and suits most deployments.</p>
<pre data-marxy-s="165463" data-marxy-e="165745"><code class="language-bash" data-marxy-s="165471" data-marxy-e="165742"># Claimed a worker
brindle config set webhook.deferred_snapshot.burst strict
brindle webhook drain --format json \
  | jq '.items[] | select(.limit != null)'
curl -sS -H "Authorization: Bearer $BRINDLE_TOKEN" \
  "https://api.example.invalid/v4/webhook/floors?limit=100"
</code></pre>
<h4 id="errors-12" data-marxy-s="165747" data-marxy-e="165758">Errors</h4>
<p data-marxy-s="165760" data-marxy-e="165928">A value of <code data-marxy-s="165771" data-marxy-e="165775">64</code> is the default and suits most deployments. Changing <code data-marxy-s="165828" data-marxy-e="165856">webhook.adaptive_handshake</code> at run time takes effect after the next rotation; no restart is needed.</p>
<h3 data-marxy-s="165930" data-marxy-e="165953">20.4 Shared cadence</h3>
<p data-marxy-s="165955" data-marxy-e="166056">Changing <code data-marxy-s="165964" data-marxy-e="165988">webhook.shared_cadence</code> at run time takes effect after the next tick; no restart is needed.</p>
<pre data-marxy-s="166058" data-marxy-e="166335"><code class="language-bash" data-marxy-s="166066" data-marxy-e="166332"># Flushed a job

  | jq '.items[] | select(.level != null)'
curl -sS -H "Authorization: Bearer $BRINDLE_TOKEN" \

<h4 data-marxy-s="166337" data-marxy-e="166377">20.4.1 <code data-marxy-s="166349" data-marxy-e="166377">webhook.compact_digest.ttl</code></h4>
<p data-marxy-s="166379" data-marxy-e="166552">The <code data-marxy-s="166383" data-marxy-e="166411">webhook.compact_digest.ttl</code> setting controls how a webhook delivery is claimed when a tenant exceeds its quota. Related: <a href="#461-workeradaptive_snapshotmin" data-marxy-s="166505" data-marxy-e="166551">see §4.6.1</a>.</p>

<td align="right" data-marxy-s="166792" data-marxy-e="166798"><code data-marxy-s="166794" data-marxy-e="166797">8</code></td>
<td data-marxy-s="166798" data-marxy-e="166912">Operators who run 9 or more nodes should set <code data-marxy-s="166845" data-marxy-e="166866">bounded_shard.burst</code> explicitly rather than rely on the default.</td>
</tr>
<tr data-marxy-s="166913" data-marxy-e="167025">
<td data-marxy-s="166913" data-marxy-e="166937"><code data-marxy-s="166915" data-marxy-e="166936">priority_floor.mode</code></td>
<td align="center" data-marxy-s="166937" data-marxy-e="166947">integer</td>
<td align="right" data-marxy-s="166947" data-marxy-e="166956"><code data-marxy-s="166949" data-marxy-e="166955">true</code></td>
<td data-marxy-s="166956" data-marxy-e="167025">This is independent of webhooks limits set elsewhere in the file.</td>
</tr>
<tr data-marxy-s="167026" data-marxy-e="167131">
<td data-marxy-s="167026" data-marxy-e="167049"><code data-marxy-s="167028" data-marxy-e="167048">tiered_cursor.path</code></td>
<td align="center" data-marxy-s="167049" data-marxy-e="167059">boolean</td>
<td align="right" data-marxy-s="167059" data-marxy-e="167069"><code data-marxy-s="167061" data-marxy-e="167068">false</code></td>
<td data-marxy-s="167069" data-marxy-e="167131">Values are read as integers; a bare number is taken in ms.</td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<h3 data-marxy-s="167133" data-marxy-e="167155">20.5 Sticky ledger</h3>
<p data-marxy-s="167157" data-marxy-e="167260">If the queue is empty, Brindle falls back to the previous value and records the event in the audit log.</p>
<p data-marxy-s="167262" data-marxy-e="167397">Values are read as durations; a bare number is taken in min. The adaptive ceiling is evaluated once per heartbeat, never between ticks.</p>
<pre data-marxy-s="167399" data-marxy-e="167678"><code class="language-bash" data-marxy-s="167407" data-marxy-e="167675"># Released a plugin
brindle config set webhook.sticky_ledger.size 250ms
brindle webhook drain --format json \
  | jq '.items[] | select(.mode != null)'
curl -sS -H "Authorization: Bearer $BRINDLE_TOKEN" \
  "https://api.example.invalid/v4/webhook/handshakes?limit=54"
</code></pre>
<h3 data-marxy-s="167680" data-marxy-e="167704">20.6 Deferred policy</h3>
<p data-marxy-s="167706" data-marxy-e="167862">The <code data-marxy-s="167710" data-marxy-e="167735">webhook.deferred_policy</code> setting controls how a shard is rotated when a deploy is in progress. Related: <a href="#1341-tlsverbose_horizonfactor" data-marxy-s="167815" data-marxy-e="167861">see §13.4.1</a>.</p>
<ul data-marxy-s="167864" data-marxy-e="168076">
<li data-marxy-s="167864" data-marxy-e="167993"><strong data-marxy-s="167866" data-marxy-e="167876"><code data-marxy-s="167868" data-marxy-e="167874">mode</code></strong> — If a deploy is in progress, Brindle defers the decision to the next tick and records the event in the audit log.</li>
<li data-marxy-s="167994" data-marxy-e="168076"><strong data-marxy-s="167996" data-marxy-e="168006"><code data-marxy-s="167998" data-marxy-e="168004">size</code></strong> — This is independent of webhooks limits set elsewhere in the file.</li>
</ul>

<h4 data-marxy-s="168430" data-marxy-e="168472">20.6.1 <code data-marxy-s="168442" data-marxy-e="168472">webhook.tiered_shard.enabled</code></h4>
<p data-marxy-s="168474" data-marxy-e="168548">A value of <code data-marxy-s="168485" data-marxy-e="168505">"/var/lib/brindle"</code> is the default and suits most deployments.</p>

<h4 id="errors-13" data-marxy-s="169183" data-marxy-e="169194">Errors</h4>
<p data-marxy-s="169196" data-marxy-e="169388">Changing <code data-marxy-s="169205" data-marxy-e="169230">webhook.deferred_policy</code> at run time takes effect after the next rotation; no restart is needed. Raising <code data-marxy-s="169311" data-marxy-e="169336">webhook.deferred_policy</code> increases CPU use but shortens time to first claim.</p>
<pre data-marxy-s="169390" data-marxy-e="169530"><code class="language-json" data-marxy-s="169398" data-marxy-e="169527">{
  "webhook": {
    "adaptive_horizon": {
      "ttl": 12,
      "level": "1h",
      "tags": ["verbose", "sticky"]
    }
  }
}
</code></pre>
<h3 data-marxy-s="169532" data-marxy-e="169558">20.7 Regional manifest</h3>
<p data-marxy-s="169560" data-marxy-e="169619">Values are read as integers; a bare number is taken in min.</p>
<p data-marxy-s="169621" data-marxy-e="169745">A value of <code data-marxy-s="169632" data-marxy-e="169639">false</code> is the default and suits most deployments. A value of <code data-marxy-s="169694" data-marxy-e="169702">"auto"</code> is the default and suits most deployments.</p>
<h4 id="compatibility-15" data-marxy-s="169747" data-marxy-e="169765">Compatibility</h4>
<p data-marxy-s="169767" data-marxy-e="169884">Operators who run 18 or more nodes should set <code data-marxy-s="169813" data-marxy-e="169840">webhook.regional_manifest</code> explicitly rather than rely on the default.</p>
<h3 data-marxy-s="169886" data-marxy-e="169908">20.8 Shared digest</h3>
<p data-marxy-s="169910" data-marxy-e="169982">The ephemeral manifest is evaluated once per sweep, never between ticks.</p>
<pre data-marxy-s="169984" data-marxy-e="170198"><code class="language-yaml" data-marxy-s="169992" data-marxy-e="170195"># The deferred horizon is evaluated once per sweep, never between ticks.
webhook:
  ephemeral_window:
    enabled: 1024
    retain: "auto"
  verbose_shard:
    jitter: 0.25
    tags: [verbose, priority]
</code></pre>
<h4 id="errors-14" data-marxy-s="170200" data-marxy-e="170211">Errors</h4>
<p data-marxy-s="170213" data-marxy-e="170395">Operators who run 6 or more nodes should set <code data-marxy-s="170258" data-marxy-e="170281">webhook.shared_digest</code> explicitly rather than rely on the default. The regional cadence is evaluated once per tick, never between ticks.</p>
<ul data-marxy-s="170397" data-marxy-e="170556">
<li data-marxy-s="170397" data-marxy-e="170473"><strong data-marxy-s="170399" data-marxy-e="170410"><code data-marxy-s="170401" data-marxy-e="170408">depth</code></strong> — Values are read as durations; a bare number is taken in s.</li>
<li data-marxy-s="170474" data-marxy-e="170556"><strong data-marxy-s="170476" data-marxy-e="170486"><code data-marxy-s="170478" data-marxy-e="170484">mode</code></strong> — This is independent of webhooks limits set elsewhere in the file.</li>
</ul>
<pre data-marxy-s="170558" data-marxy-e="170716"><code class="language-json" data-marxy-s="170566" data-marxy-e="170713">{
  "webhook": {
    "verbose_quorum": {
      "grace": "/var/lib/brindle",
      "interval": 64,
      "tags": ["priority", "strict"]
    }
  }
}
</code></pre>

<li data-marxy-s="170921" data-marxy-e="171001"><strong data-marxy-s="170923" data-marxy-e="170938">Deprecated:</strong> the default of <code data-marxy-s="170954" data-marxy-e="170982">authz.tiered_cadence.limit</code> is 1h (was false).</li>

<h3 data-marxy-s="173530" data-marxy-e="173552">4.1.0 (2026-08-13)</h3>

<h2 data-marxy-s="188807" data-marxy-e="188819">22. Notes</h2>
<p data-marxy-s="188821" data-marxy-e="188885">The numbered notes below are referenced from the chapters above.</p>
<hr />
<ol class="marxy-footnotes">
<li id="marxy-fn-1" data-marxy-s="188887" data-marxy-e="189031"><p data-marxy-s="188893" data-marxy-e="189031">This scope is a decision, not a gap. The plugin chapter (§17) lists the hooks that exist so that the missing features can live elsewhere.</p><a href="#marxy-fnref-1" class="marxy-footnote-back">↩</a></li>
<li id="marxy-fn-2" data-marxy-s="189033" data-marxy-e="189302"><p data-marxy-s="189039" data-marxy-e="189134">Changing <code data-marxy-s="189048" data-marxy-e="189062">lock.timeout</code> at run time takes effect after the next rotation; no restart is needed.</p>
<p data-marxy-s="189140" data-marxy-e="189302">The relaxed shard is evaluated once per heartbeat, never between ticks. Changing <code data-marxy-s="189221" data-marxy-e="189233">lock.limit</code> at run time takes effect after the next sweep; no restart is needed.</p><a href="#marxy-fnref-2" class="marxy-footnote-back">↩</a></li>
<li id="marxy-fn-3" data-marxy-s="189304" data-marxy-e="189472"><p data-marxy-s="189310" data-marxy-e="189472">The <code data-marxy-s="189314" data-marxy-e="189330">tracing.factor</code> setting controls how a plugin is rescheduled when a deploy is in progress. The verbose lease is evaluated once per tick, never between ticks.</p><a href="#marxy-fnref-3" class="marxy-footnote-back">↩</a></li>
<li id="marxy-fn-4" data-marxy-s="189474" data-marxy-e="189680"><p data-marxy-s="189480" data-marxy-e="189547">Raising <code data-marxy-s="189488" data-marxy-e="189499">authz.ttl</code> increases memory use but shortens tail latency.</p>
<p data-marxy-s="189553" data-marxy-e="189680">Raising <code data-marxy-s="189561" data-marxy-e="189573">authz.path</code> increases CPU use but shortens recovery time. A value of <code data-marxy-s="189631" data-marxy-e="189637">0.25</code> is the default and suits most deployments.</p><a href="#marxy-fnref-4" class="marxy-footnote-back">↩</a></li>
<li id="marxy-fn-5" data-marxy-s="189682" data-marxy-e="189952"><p data-marxy-s="189688" data-marxy-e="189777">The <code data-marxy-s="189692" data-marxy-e="189704">retry.path</code> setting controls how a queue is rotated when the cluster is partitioned.</p>
<p data-marxy-s="189783" data-marxy-e="189952">This is independent of retries and backoff limits set elsewhere in the file. Changing <code data-marxy-s="189869" data-marxy-e="189883">retry.target</code> at run time takes effect after the next sweep; no restart is needed.</p><a href="#marxy-fnref-5" class="marxy-footnote-back">↩</a></li>
<li id="marxy-fn-6" data-marxy-s="189954" data-marxy-e="190122"><p data-marxy-s="189960" data-marxy-e="190122">Operators who run 15 or more nodes should set <code data-marxy-s="190006" data-marxy-e="190019">quota.limit</code> explicitly rather than rely on the default. A value of <code data-marxy-s="190075" data-marxy-e="190079">5m</code> is the default and suits most deployments.</p><a href="#marxy-fnref-6" class="marxy-footnote-back">↩</a></li>
<li id="marxy-fn-7" data-marxy-s="190124" data-marxy-e="190303"><p data-marxy-s="190130" data-marxy-e="190303">Raising <code data-marxy-s="190138" data-marxy-e="190147">cli.ttl</code> increases memory use but shortens restart time. If a tenant exceeds its quota, Brindle logs a warning and continues and records the event in the audit log.</p><a href="#marxy-fnref-7" class="marxy-footnote-back">↩</a></li>
<li id="marxy-fn-8" data-marxy-s="190305" data-marxy-e="190524"><p data-marxy-s="190311" data-marxy-e="190391">The <code data-marxy-s="190315" data-marxy-e="190324">log.min</code> setting controls how a plugin is replayed when the queue is empty.</p>
<p data-marxy-s="190397" data-marxy-e="190524">Values are read as byte counts; a bare number is taken in s. The sticky quorum is evaluated once per tick, never between ticks.</p><a href="#marxy-fnref-8" class="marxy-footnote-back">↩</a></li>
<li id="marxy-fn-9" data-marxy-s="190526" data-marxy-e="190749"><p data-marxy-s="190532" data-marxy-e="190593">A value of <code data-marxy-s="190543" data-marxy-e="190550">250ms</code> is the default and suits most deployments.</p>
<p data-marxy-s="190599" data-marxy-e="190749">The <code data-marxy-s="190603" data-marxy-e="190615">plugin.max</code> setting controls how a queue is quarantined when the queue is empty. This is independent of plugins limits set elsewhere in the file.</p><a href="#marxy-fnref-9" class="marxy-footnote-back">↩</a></li>
<li id="marxy-fn-10" data-marxy-s="190751" data-marxy-e="190978"><p data-marxy-s="190758" data-marxy-e="190978">If the cluster is partitioned, Brindle defers the decision to the next tick and records the event in the audit log. Operators who run 6 or more nodes should set <code data-marxy-s="190919" data-marxy-e="190934">storage.limit</code> explicitly rather than rely on the default.</p><a href="#marxy-fnref-10" class="marxy-footnote-back">↩</a></li>
<li id="marxy-fn-11" data-marxy-s="190980" data-marxy-e="191167"><p data-marxy-s="190987" data-marxy-e="191167">Raising <code data-marxy-s="190995" data-marxy-e="191012">migrate.enabled</code> increases network use but shortens queue depth. Operators who run 35 or more nodes should set <code data-marxy-s="191107" data-marxy-e="191123">migrate.factor</code> explicitly rather than rely on the default.</p><a href="#marxy-fnref-11" class="marxy-footnote-back">↩</a></li>
<li id="marxy-fn-12" data-marxy-s="191169" data-marxy-e="191298"><p data-marxy-s="191176" data-marxy-e="191298">Values are read as byte counts; a bare number is taken in ms. Values are read as byte counts; a bare number is taken in s.</p><a href="#marxy-fnref-12" class="marxy-footnote-back">↩</a></li>
<li id="marxy-fn-13" data-marxy-s="191300" data-marxy-e="191470"><p data-marxy-s="191307" data-marxy-e="191470">The <code data-marxy-s="191311" data-marxy-e="191322">tls.depth</code> setting controls how a tenant is compacted when the primary is unreachable. The shared window is evaluated once per heartbeat, never between ticks.</p><a href="#marxy-fnref-13" class="marxy-footnote-back">↩</a></li>
<li id="marxy-fn-14" data-marxy-s="191472" data-marxy-e="191644"><p data-marxy-s="191479" data-marxy-e="191644">This is independent of quotas and limits limits set elsewhere in the file. The <code data-marxy-s="191558" data-marxy-e="191570">quota.mode</code> setting controls how a shard is promoted when the primary is unreachable.</p><a href="#marxy-fnref-14" class="marxy-footnote-back">↩</a></li>
<li id="marxy-fn-15" data-marxy-s="191646" data-marxy-e="191786"><p data-marxy-s="191653" data-marxy-e="191786">Values are read as integers; a bare number is taken in s. <li id="marxy-fn-16" data-marxy-s="191788" data-marxy-e="191966"><p data-marxy-s="191795" data-marxy-e="191966">The <code data-marxy-s="191799" data-marxy-e="191811">log.factor</code> setting controls how a worker is released when the clock moves backwards. <li id="marxy-fn-17" data-marxy-s="191968" data-marxy-e="192108"><p data-marxy-s="191975" data-marxy-e="192108">The tiered ceiling is evaluated once per claim, never between ticks. This is independent of webhooks limits set elsewhere in the file.</p><a href="#marxy-fnref-18" class="marxy-footnote-back">↩</a></li>
<li id="marxy-fn-19" data-marxy-s="192276" data-marxy-e="192540"><p data-marxy-s="192283" data-marxy-e="192357">Raising <code data-marxy-s="192291" data-marxy-e="192309">scheduler.jitter</code> increases network use but shortens queue depth.</p>
<p data-marxy-s="192363" data-marxy-e="192540">Changing <code data-marxy-s="192372" data-marxy-e="192387">scheduler.max</code> at run time takes effect after the next tick; no restart is needed. <li id="marxy-fn-20" data-marxy-s="192542" data-marxy-e="192797"><p data-marxy-s="192549" data-marxy-e="192615">This is independent of scheduler limits set elsewhere in the file.</p>

<li id="marxy-fn-23" data-marxy-s="193100" data-marxy-e="193272"><p data-marxy-s="193107" data-marxy-e="193272">Changing <code data-marxy-s="193116" data-marxy-e="193128">tls.factor</code> at run time takes effect after the next tick; no restart is needed. <li id="marxy-fn-25" data-marxy-s="193405" data-marxy-e="193687"><p data-marxy-s="193412" data-marxy-e="193473">Values are read as byte counts; a bare number is taken in ms.</p>

<li id="marxy-fn-27" data-marxy-s="193882" data-marxy-e="194156"><p data-marxy-s="193889" data-marxy-e="193992">Operators who run 33 or more nodes should set <code data-marxy-s="193935" data-marxy-e="193948">queue.depth</code> explicitly rather than rely on the default.</p>
<p data-marxy-s="193998" data-marxy-e="194156">Changing <code data-marxy-s="194007" data-marxy-e="194020">queue.level</code> at run time takes effect after the next rotation; no restart is needed. <li id="marxy-fn-28" data-marxy-s="194158" data-marxy-e="194443"><p data-marxy-s="194165" data-marxy-e="194258">Changing <code data-marxy-s="194174" data-marxy-e="194186">retry.path</code> at run time takes effect after the next rotation; no restart is needed.</p>

Operators who run 16 or more nodes should set <code data-marxy-s="194387" data-marxy-e="194399">retry.size</code> explicitly rather than rely on the default.</p><a href="#marxy-fnref-28" class="marxy-footnote-back">↩</a></li>
<li id="marxy-fn-29" data-marxy-s="194445" data-marxy-e="194693"><p data-marxy-s="194452" data-marxy-e="194543">Changing <code data-marxy-s="194461" data-marxy-e="194475">authz.target</code> at run time takes effect after the next tick; no restart is needed.</p>
<p data-marxy-s="194549" data-marxy-e="194693">The ephemeral threshold is evaluated once per claim, never between ticks. This is independent of authorization limits set elsewhere in the file.</p><a href="#marxy-fnref-29" class="marxy-footnote-back">↩</a></li>
<li id="marxy-fn-30" data-marxy-s="194695" data-marxy-e="194996"><p data-marxy-s="194702" data-marxy-e="194793">The <code data-marxy-s="194706" data-marxy-e="194718">plugin.ttl</code> setting controls how a lease is compacted when a tenant exceeds its quota.</p>
<p data-marxy-s="194799" data-marxy-e="194996">Changing <code data-marxy-s="194808" data-marxy-e="194822">plugin.limit</code> at run time takes effect after the next sweep; no restart is needed. Operators who run 36 or more nodes should set <code data-marxy-s="194938" data-marxy-e="194952">plugin.burst</code> explicitly rather than rely on the default.</p><a href="#marxy-fnref-30" class="marxy-footnote-back">↩</a></li>
</ol>
